## Appendix B. Frozen independent exact-readback source

The input required by this source is exactly Appendix A, saved as
frozen_exact_data.json. Its literal mathematical definitions and arithmetic
operations are included for independent review. The source was run once only.

```python
"""One frozen exact readback. No optimizer, author checker, or rank calculation."""
import json
import sys
import time
import traceback
from fractions import Fraction
from pathlib import Path

START = time.perf_counter()
ROOT = Path('/opt/danus/runtime/projects/gd-exact-profile-20260926/research/tangent_neighborhood_20261001')
RESULT = {'status': 'running', 'checks': [], 'basis_readbacks': []}


class Q:
    __slots__ = ('a', 'b')

    def __init__(self, a=0, b=0):
        self.a, self.b = Fraction(a), Fraction(b)

    @staticmethod
    def of(x):
        if isinstance(x, Q):
            return x
        if isinstance(x, list):
            return Q(*x)
        return Q(x)

    def __add__(self, other):
        other = Q.of(other)
        return Q(self.a + other.a, self.b + other.b)

    __radd__ = __add__

    def __neg__(self):
        return Q(-self.a, -self.b)

    def __sub__(self, other):
        return self + (-Q.of(other))

    def __rsub__(self, other):
        return Q.of(other) - self

    def __mul__(self, other):
        other = Q.of(other)
        return Q(self.a * other.a + 2 * self.b * other.b,
                 self.a * other.b + self.b * other.a)

    __rmul__ = __mul__

    def __truediv__(self, other):
        other = Q.of(other)
        den = other.a * other.a - 2 * other.b * other.b
        if den == 0:
            raise ZeroDivisionError('exact zero field divisor')
        return Q((self.a * other.a - 2 * self.b * other.b) / den,
                 (self.b * other.a - self.a * other.b) / den)

    def __pow__(self, exponent):
        result = Q(1)
        for _ in range(exponent):
            result = result * self
        return result

    def __bool__(self):
        return bool(self.a or self.b)

    def __eq__(self, other):
        other = Q.of(other)
        return self.a == other.a and self.b == other.b

    def encoded(self):
        return [str(self.a), str(self.b)]

    def sign(self):
        a, b = self.a, self.b
        if a == 0:
            return (b > 0) - (b < 0)
        if b == 0:
            return (a > 0) - (a < 0)
        if (a > 0) == (b > 0):
            return 1 if a > 0 else -1
        difference = a * a - 2 * b * b
        return ((a > 0) - (a < 0)) * ((difference > 0) - (difference < 0))


def encode(x):
    if isinstance(x, Q):
        return x.encoded()
    if isinstance(x, list):
        return [encode(v) for v in x]
    if isinstance(x, dict):
        return {k: encode(v) for k, v in x.items()}
    return x


def matrix(rows, cols):
    return [[Q() for _ in range(cols)] for _ in range(rows)]


def multiply(A, B):
    C = matrix(len(A), len(B[0]))
    for i, row in enumerate(A):
        for k, value in enumerate(row):
            if value:
                for j, other in enumerate(B[k]):
                    if other:
                        C[i][j] = C[i][j] + value * other
    return C


def laplacian(X):
    A = [[-x for x in row] for row in X]
    for i in range(len(X)):
        A[i][i] = sum(X[i], Q())
    return A


def check(name, condition, detail=None):
    RESULT['checks'].append({'name': name, 'passed': bool(condition), 'detail': encode(detail)})
    if not condition:
        raise AssertionError(name + ': ' + json.dumps(encode(detail)))


def main():
    data = json.loads((ROOT / 'frozen_exact_data.json').read_text())
    # Literal definitions from the mathematical manuscript, not from its checker.
    q = Q(0, 1)
    ell = 1 + q
    h = [q, Q(2), q, 1 + ell, q, Q(2), q]
    capacity = ell ** 3
    table = {
        (0, 1): Q(2), (1, 0): 2 * ell, (1, 3): 2 * ell,
        (2, 1): 2 * ell * q, (2, 3): 2 * ell ** 2 + 2 * ell * q,
        (3, 1): 2 * ell, (3, 2): 2 * ell ** 3, (3, 7): 2 * ell ** 2,
        (4, 3): 2 * ell * q, (4, 5): 2 * ell ** 2, (4, 7): 2 * ell * q,
        (5, 3): 4 * ell, (5, 4): 2 * ell ** 3, (5, 7): 2 * ell ** 3 + 4 * ell,
        (6, 3): 2 * ell * q, (6, 5): 2 * ell ** 3 * q,
        (6, 7): 2 * ell ** 4 + 2 * ell ** 3 * q + 2 * ell * q,
        (7, 3): 2 * ell, (7, 5): 2 * ell ** 3, (7, 6): 2 * ell ** 5,
    }
    pairs = [(i, j) for i in range(8) for j in range(8) if i != j]
    zeros = [ij for ij in pairs if ij not in table]
    base = matrix(8, 8)
    for (i, j), value in table.items():
        base[i][j] = value
    L0 = laplacian(base)
    T0 = [[Q(1) if i == j else (h[j] if j < i else Q()) for j in range(8)] for i in range(8)]
    s = sum(h, Q())
    omega = [-2 * x for x in h] + [2 * s]
    base_flow = [sum((L0[i][j] for i in range(8)), Q()) - omega[j] for j in range(8)]
    LT = multiply(L0, T0)
    D0 = [x * (2 - x) for x in h] + [-s * (s + 2)]
    M0 = [[(LT[i][j] + LT[j][i]) / 2 + (D0[i] if i == j else Q()) for j in range(8)] for i in range(8)]
    check('seven steps and exact base capacity', capacity == 1 + s, h)
    check('all twenty base entries positive', len(table) == 20 and all(v.sign() == 1 for v in table.values()))
    check('base original flow', all(not x for x in base_flow), base_flow)
    check('base full remainder is zero', all(not x for row in M0 for x in row), M0)

    equalities = [Q.of(v) for v in data['equality_prices']]
    check('sixteen equality prices', len(equalities) == 16)
    a, b = equalities[:8], equalities[8:]
    svec_prices = [Q.of(v) for v in data['PSD_svec']]
    check('twenty-eight svec prices', len(svec_prices) == 28)
    Z = matrix(7, 7)
    index = 0
    for j in range(7):
        for i in range(j + 1):
            value = svec_prices[index] if i == j else svec_prices[index] / q
            Z[i][j] = Z[j][i] = value
            index += 1
    stored_Z = [[Q.of(v) for v in row] for row in data['PSD_matrix']]
    check('sqrt2 column-major svec decodes the supplied matrix', Z == stored_Z, Z)
    sigma = {(i, j): Q.of(v) for i, j, v in data['all36_sign_prices']}
    check('all thirty-six original zero-pair prices, no duplicates', len(data['all36_sign_prices']) == 36 and set(sigma) == set(zeros))
    signs = [{'pair': list(ij), 'price': sigma[ij], 'sign': sigma[ij].sign()} for ij in zeros]
    check('all sign prices strictly positive in the real embedding', all(sigma[ij].sign() == 1 for ij in zeros), signs)
    box_prices = [Q.of(v) for v in data['all14_box_prices']]
    check('all fourteen box prices exactly zero', len(box_prices) == 14 and all(not v for v in box_prices), box_prices)

    lower = [[Q.of(v) for v in row] for row in data['LDL']['L']]
    pivots = [Q.of(v) for v in data['LDL']['pivots']]
    check('LDL dimensions', len(lower) == 7 and all(len(row) == 7 for row in lower) and len(pivots) == 7)
    check('LDL unit lower triangular', all(lower[i][i] == 1 for i in range(7)) and all(not lower[i][j] for i in range(7) for j in range(i + 1, 7)))
    check('all seven LDL pivots strictly positive', all(d.sign() == 1 for d in pivots), [{'value': d, 'sign': d.sign()} for d in pivots])
    ldl_residual = [[sum((lower[i][k] * pivots[k] * lower[j][k] for k in range(7)), Q()) - Z[i][j] for j in range(7)] for i in range(7)]
    check('every supplied LDL product entry equals Z', all(not v for row in ldl_residual for v in row), ldl_residual)

    # Independent V Z V^T, where V has columns e_i-e_7.
    H = matrix(8, 8)
    for i in range(7):
        for j in range(7):
            H[i][j] = Z[i][j]
        H[i][7] = -sum(Z[i], Q())
        H[7][i] = H[i][7]
    H[7][7] = sum((sum(row, Q()) for row in Z), Q())
    t0e = [sum(row, Q()) for row in T0]
    labels = [('p', i, -1) for i in range(7)] + [('Y', i, j) for i, j in pairs]
    residuals = []
    for kind, row, col in labels:
        p = [Q() for _ in range(7)]
        Y = matrix(8, 8)
        if kind == 'p':
            p[row] = Q(1)
        else:
            Y[row][col] = Q(1)
        S = sum(p, Q())
        LY = laplacian(Y)
        Tp = [[p[j] if j < i else Q() for j in range(8)] for i in range(8)]
        term1, term2 = multiply(L0, Tp), multiply(LY, T0)
        D1 = [2 * (1 - h[i]) * p[i] for i in range(7)] + [-2 * capacity * S]
        W = [[(term1[i][j] + term2[i][j] + term1[j][i] + term2[j][i]) / 2 + (D1[i] if i == j else Q()) for j in range(8)] for i in range(8)]
        op = [-2 * x for x in p] + [2 * S]
        f = [sum((LY[i][j] for i in range(8)), Q()) - op[j] for j in range(8)]
        n = [sum(W[i], Q()) for i in range(8)]
        B = [[W[i][j] - W[i][7] - W[7][j] + W[7][7] for j in range(7)] for i in range(7)]
        trace = sum((Z[i][j] * B[i][j] for i in range(7) for j in range(7)), Q())
        original_trace = sum((H[i][j] * W[i][j] for i in range(8) for j in range(8)), Q())
        svec_B = [B[i][j] if i == j else q * B[i][j] for j in range(7) for i in range(j + 1)]
        vector_trace = sum((x * y for x, y in zip(svec_prices, svec_B)), Q())
        price_sign_term = sigma.get((row, col), Q()) if kind == 'Y' else Q()
        value = trace + price_sign_term - sum((a[i] * f[i] + b[i] * n[i] for i in range(8)), Q())
        residual = value + S
        label = kind + str(row) + (',' + str(col) if kind == 'Y' else '')
        common_null_residual = sum(n, Q()) - sum((f[i] * t0e[i] for i in range(8)), Q())
        RESULT['basis_readbacks'].append(encode({'variable': label, 'S': S, 'flow': f, 'W': W, 'We': n, 'B': B, 'PSD_price': trace, 'sign_price': price_sign_term, 'stationarity_residual': residual, 'congruence_price_residual': original_trace - trace, 'svec_price_residual': vector_trace - trace, 'common_null_scalar_residual': common_null_residual}))
        check('original-variable coefficient ' + label, not residual and original_trace == trace and vector_trace == trace and not common_null_residual, residual)
        residuals.append(residual)
    RESULT['independently_rebuilt_model'] = encode({'hbar': h, 'capacity': capacity, 'Lambdabar': base, 'T0': T0, 'original_ordered_pairs': [list(ij) for ij in pairs], 'zero_pairs': [list(ij) for ij in zeros], 'Z': Z, 'H': H})
    RESULT['all_63_stationarity_residuals'] = encode(residuals)
    RESULT['stored_passed_flags_used'] = False
    RESULT['stored_stationarity_residuals_used'] = False
    RESULT['author_checker_imported'] = False
    RESULT['status'] = 'passed_exact_readback_not_formal_verification'


try:
    main()
except BaseException as error:
    RESULT['status'] = 'failed_no_retry'
    RESULT['exception'] = repr(error)
    RESULT['traceback'] = traceback.format_exc()
    traceback.print_exc()
finally:
    RESULT['internal_seconds_before_result_write'] = time.perf_counter() - START
    (ROOT / 'exact_readback_result.json').write_text(json.dumps(encode(RESULT), indent=2) + '\n')
    print(json.dumps({'status': RESULT['status'], 'checks': len(RESULT['checks']), 'basis_readbacks': len(RESULT['basis_readbacks']), 'internal_seconds_through_result_write': time.perf_counter() - START}))
sys.exit(0 if RESULT['status'] == 'passed_exact_readback_not_formal_verification' else 1)
```

## DOC-000830 — archive_full_20261010/project/research/neighborhood_formal_20261001/statement.md

来源：D:\Danus\runtime\handoffs\gd-exact-profile-20261010-full-evidence.zip!/project/research/neighborhood_formal_20261001/statement.md

原始字节数：4250；原始 SHA-256：7e2642cf2462b4c014da0d9697988b34ca49bbf05405e0e61222e3b4e5b9c1b2

证据标签：source_document_no_new_verification。以下内容原样收录，不因汇编而获得新的证明地位。

<!-- BEGIN_SOURCE_BODY DOC-000830 -->
Strict local maximality of full primitive-certificate capacity at the balanced seven-step word.

All vectors and matrices are real. Put q58=sqrt(2), lambda=1+q58, u=q58-1=1/lambda,
    hbar=(q58,2,q58,1+lambda,q58,2,q58), Abar=lambda^3.
Indices of the seven physical steps run from0 to6; indices of coefficient arrays run from0 to7. Let e be the all-ones column in R^8. For h=(h0,...,h6) in[0,infinity)^7 set s=sum_(i=0)^6 h_i and A(h)=1+s. For a real zero-diagonal8-by8 array X set L(X)=diag(Xe)-X. Define the8-by8 matrix T(h) by T(h)_ii=1, T(h)_ij=h_j for j<i, and T(h)_ij=0 for j>i. For a square real matrix K let sym(K)=(K+K^T)/2. Set
    omega(h)=(-2h0,...,-2h6,2s)^T,
    D(h)=diag(h0(2-h0),...,h6(2-h6),-s(s+2)),
    M(h,Lambda)=D(h)+sym(L(Lambda)T(h)).
Call (h,Lambda) feasible if Lambda is a real8-by8 array with Lambda_ii=0, Lambda_ij>=0 for every one of the56 ordered distinct pairs, L(Lambda)^T e=omega(h), and M(h,Lambda) is positive semidefinite on the entire R^8. No symmetry, support, rank, zero-remainder, or equality between formal gradient coordinates is assumed. Nonzero singular positive semidefinite remainders are included.

Define Lambdabar by its following twenty entries, with every other entry equal to zero:
    01:2; 10:2lambda; 13:2lambda;
    21:2lambda*q58; 23:2lambda^2+2lambda*q58; 31:2lambda;
    32:2lambda^3; 37:2lambda^2; 43:2lambda*q58;
    45:2lambda^2; 47:2lambda*q58; 53:4lambda;
    54:2lambda^3; 57:2lambda^3+4lambda; 63:2lambda*q58;
    65:2lambda^3*q58; 67:2lambda^4+2lambda^3*q58+2lambda*q58;
    73:2lambda; 75:2lambda^3; 76:2lambda^5.
Here ij denotes row i, column j. Let E58 be these twenty ordered pairs and Z0 the other36 ordered distinct pairs. The pair(hbar,Lambdabar) is feasible and M(hbar,Lambdabar)=0.

For p in R^7 and a real zero-diagonal8-by8 array Y, let S=sum_(i=0)^6 p_i, let Tp have entry p_j for j<i and zero elsewhere, and put
    op=(-2p0,...,-2p6,2S)^T,
    D1(p)=diag(2(1-hbar_i)p_i for i<7,-2Abar*S),
    W(p,Y)=D1(p)+sym(L(Lambdabar)Tp+L(Y)T(hbar)),
    f(p,Y)=L(Y)^T e-op.
The symbol f(p,Y) denotes this vector-valued flow defect, distinct from scalar objective values in the proof. Define
    C58={(p,Y): f(p,Y)=0, Y_ij>=0 for ij in Z0, W(p,Y) is positive semidefinite on R^8}.
The entries of Y on E58 and all components of p are unrestricted real numbers in this definition. Use the Euclidean norm on physical vectors and the Frobenius norm on arrays, and write
    ||(p,Y)||_* = sqrt(||p||_2^2+||Y||_F^2).

The following intermediate conclusions hold jointly. Every(p,Y) in C58 has S<=0, and S=0 in C58 holds if and only if p=0 and Y=0. Consequently Lambdabar is the unique feasible coefficient array at h=hbar. Every feasible pair with h in[1,4]^7 satisfies sum_(i!=j)Lambda_ij<=2^24. For every sequence of feasible pairs(h_k,Lambda_k) with h_k tending to hbar, the entire sequence Lambda_k tends to Lambdabar; thus this convergence holds uniformly over arbitrary choices of feasible arrays.

For arbitrary displacements p,Y, the exact full-matrix expansion is
    M(hbar+p,Lambdabar+Y)=W(p,Y)+R2(p,Y),
    R2(p,Y)=diag(-p0^2,...,-p6^2,-S^2)+sym(L(Y)Tp),
    ||R2(p,Y)||_F<=13||(p,Y)||_*^2.
Define K58={(p,Y) in C58:||(p,Y)||_*=1}. This set is nonempty and compact, and
    m58=min_((p,Y) in K58)(-sum_(i=0)^6 p_i)>0.

With c=m58/2, there exists epsilon>0 such that for EVERY h in[0,infinity)^7 and EVERY full feasible array Lambda at h, the condition ||h-hbar||_2<epsilon implies
    A(h)<=lambda^3-c*sqrt(||h-hbar||_2^2+||Lambda-Lambdabar||_F^2).
In particular, within this physical neighborhood capacity equals lambda^3 if and only if(h,Lambda)=(hbar,Lambdabar), and every such feasible array satisfies
    ||Lambda-Lambdabar||_F<=(sqrt(7)/c)||h-hbar||_2.
Only h is required epsilon-close in the hypothesis; there is no hypothesis restricting coefficient displacement or selecting a particular certificate. The coefficient estimate is relative to the base, with no assertion of pairwise Lipschitz selection or uniqueness away from the base. No numerical neighborhood radius, global capacity ceiling, or local/global gradient-descent objective minimax optimality is asserted. The original all-word exact-profile problem is not resolved by this local certificate theorem.

There are twenty-one PASS interfaces and one notation GAP, with no substantive FAIL identified. These are audit classifications, not verifier verdicts.

A01--A05: The original system and the finite dependency have the advertised scope. Write q58=sqrt(2), lambda=1+q58, u=q58-1=1/lambda, and

    hbar=(q58,2,q58,1+lambda,q58,2,q58), Abar=lambda^3.
    s=sum h_i, A(h)=1+s.
    L(X)=diag(Xe)-X.
    T_ii=1, T_ij=h_j for j<i, T_ij=0 for j>i.
    omega=(-2h0,...,-2h6,2s)^T.
    D=diag(h_i(2-h_i) for i<7,-s(s+2)).
    M=D+sym(L(Lambda)T).

Here e is the all-ones vector in R8, sym(K)=(K+K^T)/2, and Lambda is a real8-by8 array with zero diagonal. Full feasibility means all56 off-diagonal entries are nonnegative, L(Lambda)^T e=omega, and M is PSD on all of R8. The base array Lambdabar has precisely the twenty positive entries listed in frozen Section1; E58 is this support and Z0 is its36-pair complement. No gradient coordinate is identified with another.

Fact04219554e41d9750 Section10 uses exactly these definitions and the residual orientation

    Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2.
    V_h=2sum_(i<7)h_i(f_i-f7)
          +sum_(i<7)h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2-s(s+1)||g7||^2.
    V_h+sum Lambda_ij Q_ij=trace(M Gram).

The statement about all actual functions is a sufficient-certificate implication, which is enough here. The proof does not need a converse saying every primitive word possesses such an array. Section11 is a coefficient identity on the original child-gradient coordinates. Reading its algebra does not license treating an arbitrary extracted child as a feasible primitive certificate; the candidate does not do so.

The derivative variables are p in R7 and a zero-diagonal Y in R^(8-by8). Set S=sum p_i, Tp_ij=p_j for j<i and0 otherwise, op=(-2p0,...,-2p6,2S)^T,

    D1=diag(2(1-hbar_i)p_i for i<7,-2Abar*S),
    W=D1+sym(L(Lambdabar)Tp+L(Y)T(hbar)),
    f=L(Y)^T e-op.

The linearized cone is f=0, Y>=0 on Z0, W>=0 on R8. Since all hbar_i are strictly positive, no physical sign restriction is active. Since each Lambdabar entry on E58 is strictly positive, none of the corresponding Y entries has a first-order sign restriction. This is the full linearized domain needed for every feasible limiting direction, regardless of whether every point in the linearized cone integrates to an actual feasible path.

For exact flow,

    omega^T T(h)e=s^2+sum h_i^2,
    e^T D e=-s^2-sum h_i^2.

Thus e^T M e=0. For W the exact first-order identity is e^T W e=f^T T(hbar)e, which is0 when f=0. If W is PSD, the quadratic polynomial in t given by (e+t z)^T W(e+t z) has zero constant term and is nonnegative for both signs of t, so z^T W e=0 for all z. Hence We=0. Let V have columns e_i-e_7, i<7. Every x in R8 has a unique decomposition x=a e+Vz. Once We=0, x^T W x=z^T(V^T W V)z. This proves the lossless PSD equivalence, rather than replacing R8 by a smaller space without justification.

The finite identity being reused is

    -S=trace(Z V^T W V)+sum_(ij in Z0)sigma_ij Y_ij
           -alpha^T f-beta^T We.

It is a linear identity in all63 original variables. The supplied Z is positive definite, every sigma is strictly positive, and all14 historical box prices are0 according to the saved exact readback. The identity itself has no box term or dependence on a p normalization.

I read the complete AppendixB source without executing it. Its pair arithmetic represents a+b*sqrt(2) with rational a,b. Addition, multiplication, conjugate division, and the sign rule agree with that field. In the opposite-sign case, comparing a^2 with2b^2 gives the sign of a+b*sqrt(2); a nonzero rational pair cannot have a^2=2b^2. The source constructs the row-sum Laplacian and full prefix matrix from the literal twenty-entry base array. It computes the base flow and full8-by8 remainder directly, rather than reading a supplied pass flag.

The28 svec prices are decoded in upper-triangular column order, with off-diagonal entries divided by sqrt(2). The source compares the resulting matrix with every stored matrix entry. It checks the exact set of36 zero pairs, positivity of their prices, zero values for all14 box prices, unit lower-triangular LDL data, every LDL product entry, and positivity of all seven pivots. It then constructs each of seven p and fifty-six Y basis inputs, including all nonsupport coefficients, and reconstructs W, f, We, and B=V^T W V. It checks the identity, the equality of the original H:W and reduced traces for H=VZV^T, the sqrt(2)-weighted svec inner product, and e^T W e=f^T T(hbar)e.

The code does not read the historical projection rank or residuals as premises. It does not execute or check the new equality-kernel and neighborhood proof. Its exception path writes a failure status and exits nonzero; a partial run cannot acquire the final passed status through the finally block. There is no floating eigenvalue argument.

I also read the saved freeze, expected_checks.md, job_receipt.json, stdout, and the recorded check entries and basis labels in exact_readback_result.json. They report77 passing checks and63 coordinate readbacks, exit0, and empty stderr. I compared the embedded source and parsed input with the saved files as text/data-integrity operations only. The comparisons agreed. The source SHA256 is920a3c56f22daf3e73a399136178b6bedce31a08e4f79abf4aed2c3820999c1d, and input SHA256 is6b8ada87bdf542b01536e2ea4dc6336a148a7ef04c946a60b65d959c25bd0316. This establishes which existing computation was reviewed; it does not independently re-establish its arithmetic or give it fact status.

A06: On the full linearized cone, f=We=0, B=V^T W V is PSD, and the36 priced Y entries are nonnegative. The finite identity therefore yields -S>=0. If S=0, all36 sign terms vanish separately because their prices are strictly positive. Writing Z=R diag(d_i)R^T with d_i>0 gives trace(ZB)=sum_i d_i r_i^T B r_i. Every summand is nonnegative. Vanishing forces B r_i=0 by the same PSD null-vector argument. The columns of R form a basis, so B=0. With We=0 and the full decomposition above, W=0. No derivative on E58 has been incorrectly assigned a sign. Up to this point the zero-capacity kernel has not yet been shown trivial.

A07--A09: I independently rederived the three-step calculation. In this paragraph, every occurrence of the square root is explicitly q58; this is the suggested repair, not a change to the frozen file. The child word is (q58,2,q58). Its seven positive base pairs are01,10,13,21,23,31,32, with values in frozen equation(11). The additional03 derivative is a free real number.

For an arbitrary array with this eight-pair support, (LT)_02=-h2*Lambda03 and (LT)_20=0. Since the base03 entry is0, differentiating the full symmetric matrix gives

    Wloc_02=-q58*y03/2.

Consequently y03=0, independently of its sign. This also removes its possible contribution to the other differentiated entries. The original flow at0 and the full00 and01 entries are then

    y01-y10=-2r0,
    -2q58*r0-u*y01=0,

For an independent check of the00 coefficient, M00=h0(2-h0)+(1-h0)(Lambda01+Lambda03) on this support. At the base Lambda01=2 and Lambda03=0, so its r0 coefficient is2(1-q58)-2=-2q58. This directly identifies the first frozen-text notation defect.

The first two equations give y10=y01+2r0 and y01=-2q58*lambda*r0. Substitution in the third gives

    [-2q58*lambda*(u-1)+4q58]r0=4lambda*r0.

Indeed lambda(1-u)=lambda-1=q58 and q58^2=2. Thus r0=y01=y10=0. The frozen expression uses q in precisely these places, for four tokens total; no identity involving the unrelated project q was used here.

The remaining original entries and flow give

    Wloc_11=-2(1+lambda)r1-y13=0,
    2Wloc_13=-y13+y31+2lambda*r1=0,
    y13-y21-y31=-2r1.

Therefore y13=-2(1+lambda)r1, y31=y13-2lambda*r1, and y21=2(1+lambda)r1. The full12 entry is the derivative of -h2*Lambda13+(h1-1)*Lambda21, so

    0=-q58*y13-2lambda*r2+y21+2lambda*q58*r1
      =4lambda^2*r1-2lambda*r2.

It follows that r2=2lambda*r1.

For the remaining physical equation, v=T(h)^(-1)e has v0=1 and v_(i+1)=(1-h_i)v_i. Since L(Lambda)e=0 for every array with this definition of L,

    v^T M v=v^T D v=1-A(h)^2*v3^2.

The telescoping uses h_i(2-h_i)v_i^2=v_i^2-v_(i+1)^2 and the terminal coefficient1-A^2. At the base A=lambda^2, v3=-u^2, and M=0. Differentiating the left side gives v^T Wloc v because the two terms involving v' multiply M=0. Differentiating the right side gives

    (r0+r1+r2)/lambda^2+lambda(r0+r2)+r1=0.

This can also be checked from v3'/v3=lambda(r0+r2)+r1. With r0=0 and r2=2lambda*r1, the coefficient of r1 is

    (1+2lambda)/lambda^2+2lambda^2+1>0.

Hence r1=r2=0. The displayed entry and flow equations give y13=y31=y21=0. Entry22 gives y21-u*y23=0, hence y23=0, and flow at2 gives y21+y23-y32=0, hence y32=0. Every one of the eight coefficient derivatives and all three physical derivatives is zero. This argument is linear algebra on four independent formal coordinates and uses no child feasibility assumption.

A10--A11: The central-cut extraction is legitimate only after the equality reduction, and the candidate observes that order. At that point Y vanishes off E58. The real linear families h=hbar+t p and Lambda=Lambdabar+tY have exact flow for every t and M=O(t^2) because both constant and linear matrix coefficients vanish. They need not be feasible at nonzero t.

Use P={0,1,2,3}, Bset={4,5,6,7}, a=1+h0+h1+h2, b=1+h4+h5+h6, d=h3-1. The only crossing from P to Bset is N=Lambda37. The only reverse crossings are mu_j=Lambda_(j,3), j=4,5,6,7. This is derived support, not a restriction imposed on the initial full cone.

The PP subarray LambdaL has original child flow at0,1,2 because no crossing touches these indices. Its terminal child flow follows from the zero sum of its row-minus-column sums. For columns in P, T3-T7 vanishes except at column3, where it is1-h3=-d. Thus the only crossing contribution to the PP matrix block is -dN at33. The parent diagonal at3 is1-d^2 while the child terminal diagonal is1-a^2. This gives exactly

    M_PP=M_left+(a^2-d^2-dN)e3e3^T.

All derivatives of M_left except possibly33 are zero. Child flow implies e^T M_left e=0 at every t, including for signed arrays. Differentiating forces the remaining33 derivative to vanish. Base values have zero child remainder, so M_left=O(t^2) and a^2-d^2-dN=O(t^2).

Let eP be the indicator of P. On Bset, T eP equals a+d, while its value at3 is a. Right-internal rows of L annihilate this constant on Bset, leaving (LT eP)_j=d mu_j. On Bset, L^T eP is supported only at7, with value -N. Multiplication by T^T gives -Nh_j at j=4,5,6 and -N at7. D eP vanishes there. Hence

    2(MeP)_j=d mu_j-Nh_j, j=4,5,6,
    2(MeP)_7=d mu7-N.

All signs here follow from the original oriented row-minus-column flow. Since d has positive base value lambda,

    mu_j=(N/d)h_j+O(t^2), mu7=N/d+O(t^2),
    sum_(j in Bset)mu_j=Nb/d+O(t^2).

Summing the exact original flow over P gives outgoing-minus-incoming N-sum mu=-2(a+d). Thus

    N(b-d)=2d(a+d)+O(t^2).

Multiplication by d and substitution of dN=a^2-d^2+O(t^2), followed by division by the nonzero a+d, gives

    (a-d)(b-d)=2d^2+O(t^2),
    ab-d(a+b)-d^2=O(t^2).

The division is harmless near the base; no uniformity over p,Y is needed in this fixed-direction kernel calculation. The saturated bridge relation is a necessary first-order consequence, not a global restriction on arbitrary feasible arrays.

A12--A14: For positive a,b near lambda^2, define dhat as the positive root of dhat(a+b+dhat)=ab, Cjoin=a+b+dhat, and kappa=(Cjoin/b)^2. These are smooth near the base, and

    ab-d(a+b)-d^2=-(d-dhat)(a+b+d+dhat)

shows d-dhat=O(t^2). The denominator has positive base value. As a/dhat=Cjoin/b, the left-block relation gives

    N=dhat(kappa-1)+O(t^2),
    mu_j=(kappa-1)h_j+O(t^2), mu7=kappa-1+O(t^2).

Define the right child array on Bset by

    LambdaR_ij=Lambda_ij/kappa
       -(1-1/kappa)h_i*1_(i<7,j=7), i!=j,

and give it zero diagonal. The RR subarray flow at a nonterminal right index j is -2h_j-mu_j, since the additional original outgoing edge is mu_j and there is no P-to-j edge. After the scaling and subtraction, the child flow is

    (-2h_j-mu_j)/kappa-(1-1/kappa)h_j
       =-2h_j+O(t^2).

Summing supplies its terminal flow. The sign of a child coefficient, particularly its03 derivative, is unrestricted; the calculation never turns this algebraic child into an assumed feasible certificate.

The full primitive composition identity in accepted04219554e41d9750 has

    V_parent=V_left+kappa V_right-(kappa-1)Ejoin,
    Ejoin=sum_(j=4)^6 h_j Q_(j,3)+Q_(7,3)
            +dhat Q_(3,7)+sum_(j=4)^6 h_j Q_(j,7).

The terminal-right subtraction in LambdaR is exactly what restores the last sum in Ejoin when the parent coefficients are reconstructed. The crossings reconstructed with dhat and kappa agree with the actual original crossings through first order, and the bridge agrees through first order. Internal parent arrays are unchanged.

For completeness, allowing a child flow defect xi=L(Lambda)^T e-omega does not silently invoke the exact-flow formula. Collecting coefficients gives

    V+sum Lambda_ij Q_ij
       =trace(M Gram)+sum_i xi_i(f_i-||g_i||^2/2).

The value coefficient is xi_i. The squared-gradient correction is -xi_i/2 because the squared-difference part contributes sym(L)-diag(L^T e)/2. Consequently an O(t^2) child flow defect has no effect on either the constant or linear full matrix coefficient. Applying the algebraic join identity yields

    M_parent=M_left direct-sum kappa M_right+O(t^2).

This is a direct sum on P and Bset, not a quotient that identifies any gradients. Thus M_right=O(t^2). Both children have the correct base flow, zero base remainder, correct derivative flow, and zero derivative remainder.

The original RR pairs are45,47,54,57,65,67,75,76. Under local indices0,1,2,3 they are01,03,10,13,21,23,31,32. The subtraction affects only the three actual terminal columns47,57,67. At the base kappa=lambda^2, the local03 entry is

    (2lambda*q58)/lambda^2-(2/lambda)q58=0.

The seven other entries are exactly the three-step base array. The local13 entry loses the term4/lambda, and the local23 entry loses2q58/lambda, as displayed in the frozen text. This is why the signed03 extension is necessary and sufficient.

Apply the independently checked three-step calculation to each child derivative. It kills p0,p1,p2,p4,p5,p6, LambdaL', and LambdaR'. Differentiating ab-d(a+b)-d^2=O(t^2) with a'=b'=0 gives

    -(2lambda^2+2lambda)p3=0,

so p3=0. Then dhat'=kappa'=0. The crossing formulas give N'=mu_j'=0, and the exact right reconstruction

    Lambda_ij=kappa LambdaR_ij+(kappa-1)h_i*1_(i<7,j=7)

gives every RR derivative zero. The PP derivatives and all other original coefficients have already been dealt with. Hence

    f=0, W=0, Y|Z0=0 implies p=0,Y=0.

Combined with the positive-price equality reduction, this proves the intended zero-capacity linearized kernel statement without a machine rank calculation or a hidden lossless-child hypothesis.

A15: Base uniqueness does not assume the desired neighborhood theorem. If Lambda is any feasible array at exactly hbar, set p=0 and Y=Lambda-Lambdabar, with no smallness assumption on Y. Flow is linear, and M is affine in Lambda at fixed h, so

    f(0,Y)=0, W(0,Y)=M(hbar,Lambda)>=0.

On every zero base entry Y=Lambda>=0. Thus (0,Y) lies in the full linearized cone and has S=0. The kernel calculation forces Y=0. Nonzero or singular remainders would have been included in this argument and are thereby excluded at the base as a consequence, not as an assumption.

A16--A17: The coefficient bound is uniform in the choice of Lambda. The cube h_i in[1,4] contains hbar in its interior. For the one scalar quadratic F(x)=k x^2/2 with k=1/8 and x0=1, actual GD yields

    x_i=product_(j<i)(1-h_j/8),
    1/2<=1-h_j/8<=7/8.

All positions are positive and strictly decreasing. For i<j, i<=6 and

    x_i-x_j>=x_i-x_(i+1)=(h_i/8)x_i
                >=(1/8)2^(-6)=2^(-9).

The reversed ordered pair has the same squared difference. Direct substitution, not merely a generic interpolation assertion, gives

    Q_ij=-k(1-k)(x_i-x_j)^2/2
         =-7(x_i-x_j)^2/128<=-7/2^25

for all56 ordered distinct pairs. The function is convex, has Lipschitz gradient at most1, has minimizer0, and uses the actual query sequence. There are no coincident queries or untested ordered residuals.

On this trajectory h_i(1-h_i)g_i^2<=0 and the terminal square term in V_h is nonpositive. Also f_i-f7<=f_i<=k/2, while s<=28. Therefore V_h<=28k=7/2. The accepted identity and PSD give

    0<=trace(M Gram)=V_h-sum Lambda_ij(-Q_ij).

Both the minus sign and the orientation matter. Combining this with Lambda>=0 gives

    (7/2^25)sum Lambda_ij
       <=sum Lambda_ij(-Q_ij)<=V_h<=7/2,

and hence sum Lambda_ij<=2^24. Nonnegativity converts this into a bound on every entry and on the full Frobenius norm. This bounds the entire56-variable array, including cycles or other possible unbounded flow directions. No Slater condition, dual attainment, or strong regularity has been used.

Now suppose h_k->hbar, selecting any feasible Lambda_k for each k. Eventually the same cube bound applies. A subsequence of the arrays converges in a finite-dimensional compact box. Entrywise nonnegativity and zero diagonal are closed; flow and M are continuous; every quadratic form of the limit matrix is the limit of nonnegative forms. The limit is a feasible base certificate, hence Lambdabar. If convergence were not uniform over all selections, one could select a sequence with coefficient displacement bounded below by one fixed positive number; a convergent subsequence would contradict base uniqueness. This proves exactly the needed all-selection quantifier. It proves no rate at this stage and does not assume that ||Lambda-Lambdabar||/||h-hbar|| is bounded.

A18--A20: For finite displacements p=h-hbar and Y=Lambda-Lambdabar, the full formula is polynomial of total degree at most2, so

    M(h,Lambda)=W(p,Y)+R2(p,Y),
    R2=diag(-p0^2,...,-p6^2,-S^2)+sym(L(Y)Tp).

The expansion includes every quadratic coefficient-step cross term. It remains exact for sequences or nonlinear selections with no differentiability. Exact flow remains f=0; all36 zero-base signs remain Y>=0. The20 positive-base signs impose no additional limiting restriction, but are still part of the original finite feasibility system.

For a=||p||_2 and b=||Y||_F, each row-sum estimate gives ||L(Y)||_F<=(sqrt(7)+1)b, and the prefix formula gives ||Tp||_F<=sqrt(7)a. The diagonal remainder norm is at most a^2+S^2<=8a^2. Thus

    ||R2||_F<=8a^2+(7+sqrt(7))ab<=13(a^2+b^2).

For example, the last inequality follows from 7+sqrt(7)<10 and 2ab<=a^2+b^2. This is a uniform bound on the full joint unit sphere.

The cone C58 is closed in the finite-dimensional space of seven physical and56 off-diagonal variables. Its unit section K58 uses the norm

    ||(p,Y)||_* = sqrt(||p||_2^2+||Y||_F^2).

The stated nonemptiness path is valid. For x=q58-t, 0<=t<q58, the singleton multipliers2 and2(1+x) have exact flow and remainder

    (2-x^2)[[1,-1],[-1,1]],

which is PSD. Its capacity is1+x. Joining equal singleton capacities has d=u(1+x), child capacity lambda(1+x), and right scaling lambda^2. Joining the resulting equal children has d=1+x and the same right scaling. The resulting physical word is

    (x,1+u(1+x),x,2+x,x,1+u(1+x),x).

The accepted join gives full original feasibility and differentiable coefficients; at t=0 it reconstructs Lambdabar. Its physical derivative is

    p=(-1,-u,-1,-1,-1,-u,-1),
    S=-(5+2u)=-lambda^2.

It is nonzero. The derivative of the nonnegative coefficient entries at zero-base positions is nonnegative for this one-sided feasible path, and dividing its PSD remainder by positive t gives a PSD limiting W. Hence its derivative belongs to C58, proving K58 nonempty.

The equality-kernel calculation shows -S>0 at every point of K58. Compactness therefore gives a strictly positive attained minimum m58=min_K58(-S). Defining c=m58/2 is legitimate and requires no numerical estimate of m58.

Suppose no sufficiently small joint neighborhood satisfies -S>=c||(p,Y)||_* for all feasible pairs. Then there exist nonzero feasible displacements with t_k=||(p_k,Y_k)||_*->0 and -S_k<c t_k. Their normalized pairs have a convergent subsequence to a unit pair. Flow and the36 active signs pass to the limit. The exact remainder formula gives

    M(hbar+p_k,Lambdabar+Y_k)/t_k
       =W(p_k/t_k,Y_k/t_k)+t_k R2(p_k/t_k,Y_k/t_k)>=0.

The norm of the second term is at most13t_k, so the limit W is PSD on all of R8. The limiting unit pair is in K58, while its objective derivative satisfies -S<=c=m58/2. This contradicts the definition of m58. A positive joint radius r58 therefore exists.

This contradiction uses only necessity of the linearized conditions for limiting feasible displacements, not a constraint qualification or integration of every cone direction. It handles singular PSD faces by closedness. It does not assume a nonzero first physical derivative. If coefficient displacement dominates physical displacement, the limiting p can be0 while Y has unit norm; the full kernel result excludes the resulting zero-capacity cone vector just as required.

A21--A22: Uniform convergence of every feasible Lambda as h->hbar converts the joint radius into an epsilon in physical space only. More explicitly, choose epsilon small enough that ||h-hbar||<epsilon implies both the cube condition and ||(h-hbar,Lambda-Lambdabar)||_*<r58 for every feasible choice of Lambda. Existence follows by the sequential contradiction already proved; it is not assumed as continuity of a selected map.

The intended resulting theorem is precisely this: there are epsilon>0 and c>0 such that every h in[0,infinity)^7 and every real zero-diagonal Lambda with all56 original nonnegative entries, original flow, and full R8 PSD remainder satisfy, whenever ||h-hbar||_2<epsilon,

    1+sum_(i=0)^6 h_i
       <=lambda^3-c*sqrt(||h-hbar||_2^2+||Lambda-Lambdabar||_F^2).

Its equality case at capacity lambda^3 is exactly the base pair. For every pair in this neighborhood,

    c||Lambda-Lambdabar||_F
       <=lambda^3-A(h)
       =-sum_i(h_i-hbar_i)
       <=sqrt(7)||h-hbar||_2,

which proves the claimed base-relative coefficient bound. This provides no comparison of ||Lambda1-Lambda2|| with ||h1-h2|| for two arbitrary nearby selections and does not assert certificate uniqueness away from hbar. The coefficient c is not claimed optimal, and no computable numerical radius is supplied.

The hostile cases were checked against the actual assumptions. A signed extracted03 coefficient is forced to vanish by an original entry, not by a hidden sign. An unbounded family of nearby nonnegative original coefficients is ruled out by the same actual quadratic residual bound. A different feasible base array is ruled out by an exact affine cone vector, not local isolation assumed in advance. The complete static wrapper chooses the fixed conjugate interpolant only

JOINT UNVERIFIED exact curved-memory screen; no accepted mathematical result is created.

Target claim refuted: on the specified unchanged ruler words, paying every consecutive directed smooth-convex interpolation row and the initial-to-terminal two-row cycle suffices to pay all original finite rows for the curved retained-terminal law. Optimum rows are NOT part of the claimed sufficient hypotheses here.

Complete construction. For each integer m>=1536 set u=1/(4m), lambda=1+sqrt(2), c=lambda(1+u), beta=1-u, a=beta/c, H_0=(), H_(j+1)=(H_j,1+a*c^j,H_j), using the same enclosing m in every child. Let h=H_m, n=2^m-1, ell=(n-1)/2, b_r=h_r, A=sum_(r<ell)b_r=S_(m-1), B=h_ell=1+a*c^(m-1), and z_r=sum_(t=r)^(ell-1)b_t/A. Thus z_0=1, d_r=b_r/A, z_r-z_(r+1)=d_r, and E=sum_(r<ell)b_r*z_r in(0,A). The word is a palindrome.

In an orthonormal real plane(e,u_ret) choose
v_r=2e+z_r*u_ret/2 for0<=r<ell,
v_ell=e,
v_(n-1-r)=-sqrt(4-2z_r^2)*e-z_r*u_ret for0<=r<ell,
v_n=v_(n-1)=-sqrt(2)*e-u_ret.
For any sigma>0 fix X=6e-u_ret, D_r=sum_(t<r)h_t*v_t, x_r=X-sigma*D_r and g_r=sigma*v_r. Every original physical GD update holds exactly. The full Gram is a real planar Gram, hence PSD. If zeros are inserted, copy the complete jet at each repeated point, including the terminal gradient.

Choose one common value array as follows:
p_t^lo=h_t*<v_(t+1),v_t>+||v_(t+1)-v_t||^2/2,
p_t^hi=h_t*||v_t||^2-||v_(t+1)-v_t||^2/2,
p_t=(p_t^lo+p_t^hi)/2,
f_r=tau+sigma^2*sum_(t=r)^(n-1)p_t,
for any ONE real tau. This is the same midpoint convention as the full joint draft, whose specific tau is its optimum-interval midpoint. No conclusions about optimum rows are presumed.

For Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2, direct expansion yields
[Q_st+Q_ts]/sigma^2=||v_t-v_s||^2+<v_t-v_s,D_t-D_s>.
Each consecutive directed residual equals one half of the consecutive two-row charge. Thus the adjacent-directed sufficiency test reduces to the charges computed below.

Elementary exact word bounds. We have2<c<5/2, beta>7/8, 7/20<a<sqrt(2)-1, and every h_t>5/4. Put d=c-2. Then2/5<d<1/2 and0<d-a<3/20: indeed c(c-2)-beta=(2lambda^2-2lambda+1)u+lambda^2*u^2>0. The exact mass formula is
d*S_j=a*c^j+(d-a)*2^j-d.
For j>=4, B_j=1+a*c^j>1+2^j/4>3 and
S_j>(B_j-(c-1))/d>2(B_j-3/2)>B_j.
Consequently A>B, S_(m-2)>1+a*c^(m-2), A=2S_(m-2)+1+a*c^(m-2)>3 times the largest actual step in H_(m-1), so every d_r<1/3. Also A>200. Since(d-a)/a<3/7 and c>2, d*A<(10/7)B, giving B>(7/25)A.

All consecutive directed rows pass. For consecutive early gradients, the charge is
(d_r/4)(d_r-h_t*z_r)<0.
At the last early query to the middle, with z=b_0/A<1/3 and actual step b_0>5/4, it is
1+z^2/4-b_0(2+z^2/4)<0.
The middle step B>4 gives charge
(1+sqrt(4-2z^2))^2+z^2-B(1+sqrt(4-2z^2))<0,
because division by its positive1+sqrt(...) bounds the first part by3+1/9<4.
For any consecutive late return let old z, new w=z+d, actual step b>5/4, and L_z=sqrt(4-2z^2), L_w=sqrt(4-2w^2), alpha=L_z-L_w. Its exact charge is
alpha^2+d^2+b(-L_z*alpha+z*d).
The identity alpha(L_z+L_w)=2d(w+z) gives
-L_z*alpha+z*d=-dw-d(w+z)alpha/(L_z+L_w)<=-dw,
(alpha/d)^2<=2w^2,
so charge<=d^2(1+2w^2-bw/d)<0.
For w<=1/3 use1+2w^2<=11/9<5/4 and w/d>=1.
For w>=1/3 use d<1/3 and
1+2w^2<=11w/3<15w/4<3bw<bw/d,
where11w/3-1-2w^2=2(w-1/3)(3/2-w)>=0.
The final gradient change is0, so its directed rows are0. The midpoint value array therefore pays every adjacent directed row in both orientations.

The initial-terminal cycle passes too. Its exact charge is
(2+sqrt(2))^2+9/4-(2+sqrt(2))*D_long+3E/4,
where D_long=B+sum b_r*(2-sqrt(4-2z_r^2))>=B.
Using E<A, B>(7/25)A and3<2+sqrt(2)<7/2 bounds it above by
15-(9/100)A<0.

The missing original remote cycle fails. The original central query ell has v_ell=e and its FULL remaining physical displacement is
D_n-D_ell=(B-T)*e-E*u_ret,
T=sum_(r<ell)b_r*sqrt(4-2z_r^2)>=sqrt(2)A.
Its terminal gradient is(-sqrt(2),-1), so the ORIGINAL central-terminal charge is
[Q_(ell,n)+Q_(n,ell)]/sigma^2
=(1+sqrt(2))^2+1+(1+sqrt(2))(T-B)+E>A>0,
because B<A and(1+sqrt(2))(sqrt(2)-1)=1.
Hence at least one of these original remote directed rows is positive for EVERY choice of the single tau, every positive sigma and every anchor. No static convex1-smooth function realizes the data, by the necessary original rows in accepted1252b8823bb5a290.

The test refutes the stated truncated finite-row sufficiency claim only. It does not claim that all optimum rows pass, supply an admissible adversarial function, or bound unrestricted W. The complete joint draft f343b740051db99f independently derives the whole construction, original optimum/value rows, true-radius guarded static wrapper and the other two laws. Neither that draft nor this counterexample has been formally verified. Mathematical programs, arithmetic replay, trajectories, recurrences and formal calls:0.

# Nonclosing terminal memory: three exact original-row screens

Status: COMPLETE JOINT ANALYTICAL DRAFT, UNVERIFIED.
Worker max2, invocation62, plan ec5d5083bc811345.
Master guidance93fb1d428186ede2; elaboration0d9770af2df0344c.
The original exact-profile target remains OPEN.

This is the ONE allocated analytical invocation after ended baseline61,
ended ceiling62/attempted ceiling63. It allocates no later round.
All mathematical work below is symbolic text. Mathematical programs,
numeric evaluation, arithmetic replay, executed trajectories/recurrences,
optimizers/solvers, benchmark DP, formal gates/fact_submit, installations,
consults and subagents: ZERO.

## 1. Scope and result

We specify THREE complete laws on the FULL given nonnegative physical word.
They genuinely retain a nonzero terminal memory, or choose an explicit
last-step-dependent terminal coefficient. No free terminal reset is used.
All returns are attached to actual positive physical steps.

The laws are:

A. Retain the final negative-memory jet, keeping word-dependent paired
   coefficients at the early queries.
B. Use constant early longitudinal coefficients, rebuild the signed
   retired memory at the actual late queries, and fit the terminal
   coefficient to the last physical two-row inequality.
C. Pay every late return locally by a curved longitudinal coefficient
   -sqrt(4-2z^2), and retain the final negative-memory jet.

On the SAME inherited-enclosing-m tau=1/4 word H_m, for EVERY m>=1536:

* A pays its initial-to-terminal and last cycles, but its FIRST physical
  two-row cycle is strictly positive.
* B pays its first, initial-to-terminal and last cycles, but its
  PENULTIMATE physical two-row cycle is strictly positive.
* C pays EVERY consecutive physical two-row cycle and its
  initial-to-terminal cycle. Its ORIGINAL central-query-to-terminal
  two-row cycle is strictly positive.

The impossibility screens hold for EVERY positive common scale, every
value array, every initial anchor and every minimizer. These are failures
of the stated coefficient laws, not bounds on unrestricted W.

A and B leave exact sum h_t*g_t=S*g_n on every word with at least four
positive steps. C leaves it on the decisive H_m words. We do NOT apply
the previous closure/HUBER argument outside its hypotheses.

We also give a completely specified static wrapper: use a law's fixed
jets only if every original directed row passes with a strictly feasible
optimum interval; otherwise choose the canonical Huber function BEFORE
the whole run. This wrapper is legal on every physical word. On H_m
each of the three wrappers is exactly the Huber fallback, with TRUE
closest radius1 and normalized payoff1/(1+2S_m). Accepted fact
5fd396eba75651e7 then gives its original benchmark ratio<237/250.
There is no stronger finite bridge or universal profile construction.

Only accepted1252b8823bb5a290 (the original interpolation inequalities
and its self-contained conjugate construction) and accepted
5fd396eba75651e7 (only its same-word Huber/benchmark comparison) are
mathematical premises. The three prior findings87225e1b68c94b70,
c47175818b62b7b9 and329c21b1c4c24404 supply orientation, not premises.
Every changed coefficient and cycle is rederived in this joint draft.
No new component below is presented as a verifier-accepted fact.

## 2. Full word, exact features, and repetition convention

Normalize smoothness to1. Let

    h=(h_0,...,h_(n-1)) in [0,infinity)^n,
    S=sum_(i<n)h_i.

List its positive physical steps at original indices

    i_0<...<i_(k-1), b_r=h_(i_r)>0,
    j(i)=#{r:i_r<i}, 0<=i<=n.

A compressed sample r is lifted to original physical sample i by r=j(i).
Thus every zero step repeats the COMPLETE point, gradient AND value,
including zeros before the first positive step and after the last one.
All original ordered rows remain in the construction.

For k<4 use the canonical Huber fallback in Section4.3. This common
fallback is not a fourth screened memory law. Henceforth k>=4 and

    ell=floor(k/2), r'=k-1-r for 0<=r<ell,
    A_half=sum_(r<ell)b_r>0,
    z_r=sum_(a=r)^(ell-1)b_a/A_half, 0<=r<ell,
    d_r=b_r/A_half.

In particular z_0=1, 0<z_r<=1, and

    z_r-z_(r+1)=d_r for r<ell-1,
    z_(ell-1)=d_(ell-1).

These are finite DEFINITIONS using the entire physical word, not an
executed recurrence. Our fixed real features are the orthonormal vectors

    e_ret=(1,0), u_ret=(0,1).

The explicitly retired suffix is z_r*u_ret, and the rth innovation being
retired is d_r*u_ret. Its exact Gram is

    <z_r*u_ret,z_s*u_ret>=z_r*z_s.

The purpose here is to change the temporal return law and pay its
cycles. There is no arbitrary PSD selection, and no claim that a Gram
change alone is progress.

The late query r' returns the SAME already specified suffix z_r, with
the explicit coefficients given next. The actual ratio is

    rho_r=b_r/b_(r')>0.

If k is odd, the middle query ell has gradient vector e_ret. Its step
mass is B_mid=b_ell. If k is even put B_mid=0 and there is no middle query.
Write

    E_suffix=sum_(r<ell)b_r*z_r.

Since ell>=2 and all b_r>0,

    0<E_suffix<A_half.                                  (1)

Indeed z_0=1 and z_1<1, and each z_r<=1.

## 3. Three full coefficient laws

For each law write its dimensionless vector as

    v_r=L_r*e_ret+T_r*u_ret, 0<=r<=k.

All coefficients are fixed from the given h before any function is run.

### 3.1 A: ratio-weighted retained endpoint

Define

    alpha_r=b_(r')/(1+b_r+b_(r')),
    beta_r=b_r/(1+b_r+b_(r')),
    alpha_0>0.

For early and paired late queries put

    v_r=e_ret+(alpha_r/alpha_0)*(e_ret+z_r*u_ret/2),
    v_(r')=e_ret-2*(beta_r/alpha_0)*(e_ret+z_r*u_ret/2). (2A)

Use v_ell=e_ret when k is odd. RETAIN the last actual jet:

    v_k=v_(k-1)
       =(1-2rho_0)*e_ret-rho_0*u_ret.                 (3A)

There is no change of gradient at the last physical step in this law.

Define the fully specified longitudinal and transverse return sums

    M_long=sum_(r<ell)b_r*alpha_r/alpha_0,
    M_trans=sum_(r<ell)b_r*alpha_r*z_r/alpha_0.

The pair identity b_r*alpha_r=b_(r')*beta_r gives the FULL displacement

    D_k=sum_(r<k)b_r*v_r
       =(S-M_long)*e_ret-M_trans*u_ret/2.             (4A)

No remote coefficient is discarded. Because ell>=2,

    M_trans<M_long.                                  (5A)

For the terminal vector in(3A), L_k=1+2T_k. If D_k=S*v_k,
its coordinates in(4A) would give M_long=M_trans, contrary to(5A).
Thus A is NONCLOSING for every stated given word.

### 3.2 B: constant early coefficients and last-step endpoint matching

For early and paired late queries put

    v_r=2e_ret+z_r*u_ret/2,
    v_(r')=(1-2rho_r)*e_ret-rho_r*z_r*u_ret.           (2B)

The middle vector, when present, is e_ret. The last preterminal vector is

    w_last=(1-2rho_0)*e_ret-rho_0*u_ret.
    Delta_end=e_ret-w_last=rho_0*(2e_ret+u_ret).

Define the explicit, word-dependent endpoint coefficient

    q_end=min{1, max{0,b_(k-1)*(1-2/(5rho_0))}},
    v_k=w_last+q_end*Delta_end.                       (3B)

A trailing zero does not trigger(3B) a second time: the full terminal
jet is set at compressed query k and copied at every repeated point.

The FULL displacement is exactly

    D_k=(S-A_half)*e_ret-E_suffix*u_ret/2.            (4B)

To obtain it, a paired longitudinal contribution is
2b_r+b_(r')*(1-2rho_r)=b_(r'), and the transverse contribution is
b_r*z_r/2-b_(r')*rho_r*z_r=-b_r*z_r/2.

Again L_k=1+2T_k. If D_k=S*v_k, the coordinate identity would imply
A_half=E_suffix, which(1) forbids. Hence B is NONCLOSING for every
stated word, independently of the chosen endpoint coefficient.

The endpoint cycle is rederived directly. The last vector change is
q_end*Delta_end, so its dimensionless charge is

    q_end^2*||Delta_end||^2
          +b_(k-1)*q_end*<Delta_end,w_last>
      =rho_0^2*q_end
             [5q_end+b_(k-1)*(2/rho_0-5)]<=0.        (5B)

If rho_0<=2/5, q_end=0 and the charge is0.
If rho_0>2/5, the chosen q_end is no greater than
b_(k-1)*(1-2/(5rho_0)); substituting proves the inequality.
This pays the ORIGINAL last positive physical step, not a substituted
terminal price or a fictitious reset.

### 3.3 C: curved longitudinal payment on every late return

Let

    ell_curve(z)=sqrt(4-2z^2), 0<=z<=1.

This is real and lies in[sqrt(2),2]. Set

    v_r=2e_ret+z_r*u_ret/2,
    v_(r')=-ell_curve(z_r)*e_ret-rho_r*z_r*u_ret.     (2C)

Use v_ell=e_ret when k is odd, and RETAIN the last vector:

    v_k=v_(k-1)=-sqrt(2)*e_ret-rho_0*u_ret.           (3C)

The longitudinal payment is not the old linear pair compensation.
It changes at each ACTUAL late query. Its full displacement is

    D_k=[2A_half-sum_(r<ell)b_(r')*ell_curve(z_r)+B_mid]*e_ret
                            -E_suffix*u_ret/2.      (4C)

On positive palindromes rho_r=1 and
S=2A_half+B_mid, so

    D_k=[B_mid+sum_(r<ell)b_r*(2-ell_curve(z_r))]*e_ret
                            -E_suffix*u_ret/2.      (5C)

Its longitudinal coordinate is positive, whereas v_k's is negative.
Thus C leaves exact D_k=S*v_k on every positive odd palindrome,
in particular every diagnostic H_m. No closure-based Huber bound is
used to screen C. Nothing in this draft asserts that an arbitrary
nonpalindromic C input cannot accidentally satisfy a closure identity.

## 4. Every point, one common value array, every original row, and static wrapper

The fixed initial anchor for each memory transcript is

    X_ret=6e_ret-u_ret, ||X_ret||^2=37.

For the definite full-h mapping choose

    sigma_h=1/(1+2S)>0.

The impossibility proofs will hold for any sigma>0, so we also state
the full arrays with an arbitrary positive sigma. This is a scope
strengthening of the screen, not an optimization performed here.

For each specified law define the finite prefix sums

    D_r=sum_(t<r)b_t*v_t,
    xhat_r=X_ret-sigma*D_r, ghat_r=sigma*v_r, 0<=r<=k,
    x_i=xhat_(j(i)), g_i=ghat_(j(i)), 0<=i<=n.        (6)

These exact position definitions imply EVERY physical GD identity
x_(i+1)=x_i-h_i*g_i. All vector coordinates and correlations are specified:

    <g_i,g_j>=sigma^2*(L_(j(i))*L_(j(j))+T_(j(i))*T_(j(j))),
    <X_ret,g_i>=sigma*(6L_(j(i))-T_(j(i))),
    <x_i,g_j>=<X_ret,g_j>
                       -sigma^2*<D_(j(i)),v_(j(j))>,
    <x_i,x_j>=37-sigma*<X_ret,D_(j(i))+D_(j(j))>
                       +sigma^2*<D_(j(i)),D_(j(j))>. (7)

The complete Gram is the Gram of real planar vectors. It is PSD
without any selection of a matrix variable.

For each compressed step t<k define its two adjacent price bounds

    p_t^lo=b_t*<v_(t+1),v_t>+||v_(t+1)-v_t||^2/2,
    p_t^hi=b_t*||v_t||^2-||v_(t+1)-v_t||^2/2,
    p_t=(p_t^lo+p_t^hi)/2,
    C_r^val=sum_(t=r)^(k-1)p_t, C_k^val=0.           (8)

These averages are DEFINED even when p_t^lo>p_t^hi. They do not imply
legality. They specify ONE value array, with no separately retuned
terminal or remote values.

For the optimum jet x_star=g_star=f_star=0 define

    U_r=sigma*<v_r,X_ret>
          -sigma^2*[C_r^val+<v_r,D_r>+||v_r||^2/2],
    L_r^opt=sigma^2*[||v_r||^2/2-C_r^val],
    U_min=min_(0<=r<=k)U_r,
    L_max=max_(0<=r<=k)L_r^opt,
    tau_val=(U_min+L_max)/2,
    fhat_r=tau_val+sigma^2*C_r^val,
    f_i=fhat_(j(i)).                                 (9)

The reused letter L_r in(7) denotes a vector coefficient; L_r^opt in(9)
is an optimum-row scalar. They are distinct and defined here.

The ORIGINAL residual orientation from accepted1252b8823bb5a290 is

    Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2.

The FULL array, including every finite/remote/terminal/optimum row, is

    Q_(i,j)/sigma^2
      =C_i^val-C_j^val+<v_i,D_i-D_j>+||v_i-v_j||^2/2,
                         0<=i,j<=k,
    Q_(r,star)=tau_val-U_r,
    Q_(star,r)=L_r^opt-tau_val.                       (10)

Original physical finite rows are(10) with their indices j(i),j(j).
Equal compressed indices give residual0. No directed row is deleted.
All directed cycle costs are exact sums of(10); their values telescope.

For ANY two compressed finite indices s<t the exact two-row charge is

    [Q_(s,t)+Q_(t,s)]/sigma^2
      =||v_t-v_s||^2+<v_t-v_s,D_t-D_s>.              (11)

For an adjacent step it becomes

    ||v_(t+1)-v_t||^2+b_t*<v_(t+1)-v_t,v_t>.        (12)

Equation(11) follows by substituting both original rows and cancelling
the values. It therefore holds for every possible common value array,
not only(9), and for any initial anchor or minimizer.

For reference, the INITIAL OPTIMUM-to-terminal path is also preserved.
Adding Q_(0,star) and every Q_(t+1,t) gives, for any legal values,

    2f_k<=2sigma*<v_0,X_ret>-sigma^2*D_path,
    D_path=||v_0||^2
           +2sum_(t<k)b_t*<v_(t+1),v_t>
           +sum_(t<k)||v_(t+1)-v_t||^2.             (13)

There is no claim of positivity or sufficiency for D_path.

### 4.1 Exact static realization when all rows pass

Use the fixed sigma_h and the fixed arrays(6)-(9).
If U_min>L_max and EVERY finite original row(10) is nonpositive, then
the optimum rows are also strictly negative. Accepted1252b8823bb5a290,
paragraph2, applied to the entire finite sample set gives ONE static
function

    K_ret(v)=max{||v||^2/2,
         max_(0<=i<=n)[<x_i,v>-f_i+||v-g_i||^2/2]},
    F_ret(x)=sup_v[<x,v>-K_ret(v)].                  (14)

This finite fixed maximum realizes all points, gradients and values.
It is finite everywhere, convex differentiable, 1-smooth and has
minimum0 at0. Its gradients are the prescribed g_i, so(6) is its
actual full-word trajectory. ALL original rows have been paid,
including incoming and outgoing terminal rows.

Moreover U_min>L_max implies tau_val>L_r^opt for every r and hence

    f_i>||g_i||^2/2.

At v=0 every nonzero-jet term in K_ret is strictly negative and the
zero-optimum term is0. Finitely many continuous quadratics then leave
K_ret(v)=||v||^2/2 in a neighborhood of0. Consequently
partial K_ret(0)={0}, so argmin F_ret={0}. The TRUE closest initial
radius is sqrt(37), and the exact payoff is

    2*tau_val/37.                                    (15)

No initial radius is replaced by1. This establishes a scalable
conditional realization for these FIXED arrays; no PSD or PEP variables
are searched. It does not assert that any diagnostic array passes.

The term(14) is not claimed to interpolate a failing array. A positive
cycle proves that NO such static interpolant exists, even if this
formula by itself still defines some unrelated smooth function.

### 4.2 No reset at a repeated zero-step terminal

All terminal jets are specified at query k after the last positive step.
Original samples after that step all use j(i)=k, so have exactly the same
point, gradient and value. There is no attempt to change the terminal
gradient later while the point is unchanged. Leading and interior
zero-step repetitions have the same property.

The two rows of a failed cycle lift to original physical rows, even
with zero padding. When a consecutive compressed cycle is used,
choose the original positive query and the point immediately after
that actual positive step; the prescribed jets equal the compressed
ones. When a remote cycle is used, choose the corresponding original
positive query and original terminal n.

### 4.3 A fully legal, static, all-word wrapper and its true radius

For each of A,B,C choose(14) only if the strict interval and every
original row test in4.1 pass. If not, or if k<4, choose the symmetric
Huber objective BEFORE starting the entire run:

    delta=1/(1+2S),
    Hub_delta(x)=x^2/2 for |x|<=delta,
                 delta*|x|-delta^2/2 for |x|>=delta,
    x_0=1.

Its unique minimum is0, its smoothness is1, and every physical sample is

    x_i=1-delta*sum_(t<i)h_t>=delta,
    g_i=delta,
    f_i=delta*x_i-delta^2/2.                          (16)

The inequality includes S=0, when delta=1 and every sample is1.
For S>0, the terminal point is (1+S)/(1+2S)>delta.
Its true closest squared radius is1 and its exact payoff is

    2f_n=1/(1+2S).                                   (17)

All finite rows are0 because the gradient is constant and the values
are affine on the visited plateau. Its optimum outgoing rows are0.
The incoming optimum rows are delta*(delta-x_i)<=0.
Thus ALL original rows pass, including repetitions and the terminal.

This wrapper defines one static finite-dimensional smooth convex
function with an attained minimum for EVERY h, with a completely
specified initial point and correct true radius in both cases.
The function is selected once from the FULL given word. There is no
splice, restart, query-dependent selection or free endpoint reset.
It is only an honest completion of a screened law; if all three fail,
it claims exactly the already known Huber witness.

For every advertised L>0 multiply the chosen objective by L and use
steps h_i/L. The points and true-distance normalized payoff are the same.
The wrapper is not an unrestricted PEP reformulation: each attempted
coefficient, scale, anchor and value has already been fixed explicitly.

## 5. Same enclosing-m diagnostic word and exact elementary bounds

For EVERY integer m>=1536 keep exactly

    lambda=1+sqrt(2), u_m=1/(4m),
    beta_word=1-u_m, c_word=lambda*(1+u_m),
    a_word=beta_word/c_word,
    H_0=(), H_(j+1)=(H_j,1+a_word*c_word^j,H_j), 0<=j<m,
    h=H_m, n_m=2^m-1.                                (18)

Each child inherits THIS same enclosing m. There is no child-local
parameter substitution or changed horizon. Every step is positive
and the word is a palindrome. Its first half is H_(m-1); thus

    k=n_m, ell=2^(m-1)-1, A_half=S_(m-1),
    B_mid=1+a_word*c_word^(m-1),
    b_0=1+a_word, b_1=1+beta_word,
    rho_r=1.

The letter b_r denotes the actual chronological step, not the level.

Here are exact bounds used below. Since sqrt(2)<29/20 and
u_m<=1/64, we have

    2<c_word<5/2, beta_word>7/8,
    7/20<a_word<sqrt(2)-1<1/2,
    5/4<b_0<sqrt(2)<3/2, b_1>7/4.                  (19)

For the upper bound on c_word,
(49/20)*(65/64)=3185/1280<5/2.
The a_word lower bound follows from (7/8)/(5/2)=7/20.

Put d_word=c_word-2. Then

    2/5<d_word<1/2, 0<d_word-a_word<3/20.            (20)

For positivity, c_word*(c_word-2)-beta_word is0 at u_m=0 and,
after expansion, is

    (2lambda^2-2lambda+1)*u_m+lambda^2*u_m^2>0.

Divide by c_word. The other inequalities follow from(19).

The exact word mass is

    S_j=2^j-1+a_word*(c_word^j-2^j)/(c_word-2),       (21)
    d_word*S_j=a_word*c_word^j
                       +(d_word-a_word)*2^j-d_word.

This is proved by substitution into S_(j+1)=2S_j+1+a_word*c_word^j
with S_0=0; no recurrence is executed.

For j>=4, put B_j=1+a_word*c_word^j. Since a_word>1/4 and c_word>2,
B_j>1+2^j/4>3. Equations(20),(21) give

    S_j>(B_j-(c_word-1))/d_word
          >2(B_j-3/2)>B_j.                          (22)

In particular

    A_half>B_mid,
    S_(m-2)>1+a_word*c_word^(m-2),
    A_half=2S_(m-2)+1+a_word*c_word^(m-2)
             >3[1+a_word*c_word^(m-2)].             (23)

The bracket is the LARGEST actual step in H_(m-1), so

    0<d_r=b_r/A_half<1/3 for every r<ell.            (24)

Also A_half>ell>200. In particular A_half>28b_0, since b_0<3/2
and ell>=63 already for m>=7.

For the initial-to-terminal C screen we will use a sharper ratio.
By(19),(20),

    (d_word-a_word)/a_word<3/7.
    d_word*A_half
      =a_word*c_word^(m-1)
          +(d_word-a_word)*2^(m-1)-d_word
      <(10/7)*a_word*c_word^(m-1)
      <(10/7)*B_mid.

Hence

    B_mid>(7/10)*d_word*A_half>(7/25)*A_half.        (25)

All these bounds are symbolic and uniform over the stated m.
They are preliminary computations WITHIN this joint unverified proof,
not separately promoted lemmas.

## 6. Law A: terminal retention pays final and remote, but first ratios increase

On(18), alpha_r=b_r/(1+2b_r) and z_1=1-b_0/A_half.
Using(19),

    alpha_0<3/8, alpha_1>7/18,
    alpha_0/alpha_1<27/28,
    z_1>27/28.                                      (26)

The last inequality uses A_half>28b_0 from Section5.
At the first two ORIGINAL physical queries,

    v_0=2e_ret+u_ret/2,
    v_1=(1+alpha_1/alpha_0)e_ret
                    +(alpha_1*z_1/(2alpha_0))*u_ret.

Write their vector difference as dL*e_ret+dT*u_ret. By(26),

    dL=alpha_1/alpha_0-1>0,
    dT=(alpha_1*z_1/alpha_0-1)/2>0.

The FIRST original two-row cycle is therefore

    [Q_(0,1)+Q_(1,0)]/sigma^2
       =dL^2+dT^2+b_0*(2dL+dT/2)>0.                (27A)

This holds for ALL values, anchors, minima and positive sigma.
It is NOT the previous simplex/affine-clock first-cycle formula.

For clarity, the changed initial-to-terminal and final cycles really
are paid here. The retained terminal vector is v_k=-e_ret-u_ret,
so v_k-v_0=-3e_ret-3u_ret/2. Substituting(4A) into(11) gives

    [Q_(0,k)+Q_(k,0)]/sigma^2
       =45/4-3(S-M_long)+(3/4)*M_trans.             (28A)

By b_0>5/4, alpha_0>5/14. Since alpha_r<1/2 and
M_trans<=M_long,

    M_long<(7/5)*A_half,
    (28A)<45/4-3(2A_half+B_mid)+(15/4)*(7/5)A_half
          =45/4-(3/4)A_half-3B_mid<0.               (29A)

The final gradient change is0, so its original last-step cycle is0.
Thus retaining the terminal jet repairs that endpoint; the price
moves to the FIRST physical query because the early coefficient
increases before its suffix memory has retired enough.

No static function can interpolate the law on(18), regardless of
whether one changes the chosen anchor or the common value convention.

## 7. Law B: fitted terminal passes, actual penultimate rebuild fails

On the palindrome(18), rho_r=1. At the first query pair,

    v_0=2e_ret+u_ret/2,
    v_1=2e_ret+(1-d_0)*u_ret/2,
    d_0=b_0/A_half.

The first ORIGINAL charge is

    [Q_(0,1)+Q_(1,0)]/sigma^2
       =(d_0/4)*(d_0-b_0)<0,                        (27B)

since A_half>1. The last endpoint rule is

    q_end=3b_0/5 in(0,1),
    v_k=(2q_end-1)e_ret+(q_end-1)u_ret.

The endpoint charge is5q_end^2-3b_0*q_end=0, as obtained directly
from(5B), not by copying the former zero-terminal formula.

The changed initial-to-terminal cycle is

    a_end=q_end-3/2 in[-3/2,-1/2],
    v_k-v_0=a_end*(2e_ret+u_ret),
    [Q_(0,k)+Q_(k,0)]/sigma^2
       =5a_end^2+a_end*[2(S-A_half)-E_suffix/2].     (28B)

The bracket is at least(3/2)A_half+2B_mid>15/2,
whereas -5a_end<=15/2. Consequently(28B)<0.

Nevertheless the last two PREterminal physical gradients rebuild the
retired memory with the wrong sign. The actual penultimate step is b_1
because the physical H_m is a palindrome. Its two query vectors are

    v_(k-2)=-e_ret-z_1*u_ret,
    v_(k-1)=-e_ret-u_ret,
    v_(k-1)-v_(k-2)=-d_0*u_ret.

Their ORIGINAL charge is

    [Q_(k-2,k-1)+Q_(k-1,k-2)]/sigma^2
       =d_0^2+b_1*z_1*d_0>0.                       (29B)

Every quantity is an actual full-word coefficient. The late direction
is not an independent innovation or a reset. The whole return
displacement(4B) and the full directed costs(10) include it.

For any other consecutive late pair r+1 -> r, with positive query
step b_(k-2-r), the palindrome charge is

    d_r^2+b_(k-2-r)*z_(r+1)*d_r>0.

Thus the defect is a full sequence of genuine late return cycles,
not an isolated unqueried-terminal mismatch. We need only(29B) to
reject every scale member at every assigned depth.

Fitting a final coefficient pays ONE physical endpoint. It cannot
pay the newly introduced earlier return cycles.

## 8. Law C: all consecutive cycles pass; a central remote cycle still fails

On(18) the late gradients are

    -ell_curve(z_r)*e_ret-z_r*u_ret.

Unlike B, the longitudinal coefficient changes at EVERY actual
late return and can pay its local transverse increase.

### 8.1 Every consecutive cycle, including first/middle/final

The early consecutive cycles have difference -d_r*u_ret/2 and charge

    (d_r/4)*(d_r-b_r*z_r)<0,                         (30C)

because z_r>=d_r and b_r>5/4.
In particular the changed FIRST cycle is exactly(27B).

The last early query has z=z_(ell-1)=b_0/A_half. Its step is b_0
and the next (middle) vector is e_ret. Its charge is

    1+z^2/4-b_0*(2+z^2/4)<0.                       (31C)

The actual CENTRAL step is B_mid. Its next vector is the youngest
late return (-ell_curve(z),-z). The charge is

    (1+ell_curve(z))^2+z^2-B_mid*(1+ell_curve(z))<0. (32C)

Indeed z<1/3, 1+ell_curve(z)<=3, B_mid>4, so dividing the expression
by the positive1+ell_curve(z) bounds its first part by
3+1/9<4<B_mid.

Now consider any consecutive late return with old z and new w=z+d,
where 0<=z<w<=1, d=d_r<1/3, and actual step b>5/4.
Put

    L_z=ell_curve(z), L_w=ell_curve(w), a=L_z-L_w>0.

The EXACT vector difference and charge are

    Delta=a*e_ret-d*u_ret,
    charge=a^2+d^2+b*(-L_z*a+z*d).                  (33C)

The curved coefficient satisfies

    a*(L_z+L_w)=2d(w+z),
    -L_z*a+z*d=-d*w-d*(w+z)*a/(L_z+L_w)<=-d*w,
    a/d=2(w+z)/(L_z+L_w)<=2w/L_w.

Hence

    (a/d)^2<=4w^2/(4-2w^2)<=2w^2,
    charge<=d^2*[1+2w^2-b*w/d].                    (34C)

If w<=1/3, then w/d>=1 and 1+2w^2<=11/9<5/4<b.
If w>=1/3, d<1/3 gives w/d>3w, and

    1+2w^2<=11w/3<15w/4<3bw,

because
11w/3-1-2w^2=2(w-1/3)(3/2-w)>=0 on[1/3,1].
Thus EVERY late consecutive charge is strictly negative.
This calculation retains the actual step b, the old longitudinal
coefficient and both current transverse coefficients.

Finally v_k=v_(k-1), so the final original cycle is0.
Consequently ALL consecutive two-row cycles have been paid.

For the SPECIFIED midpoint prices(8), each of the two consecutive
directed residuals is exactly one half of its two-row charge: the
upper residual is p_t-p_t^hi and the lower is p_t^lo-p_t, multiplied
by sigma^2. Thus ALL consecutive directed rows also pass for this
single value array. This fact supplies no remote or optimum-row
legality, and none is inferred.

### 8.2 The original initial-to-terminal cycle also passes

Let

    D_long=B_mid+sum_(r<ell)b_r*(2-ell_curve(z_r))>=B_mid.

The complete displacement is D_k=D_long*e_ret-E_suffix*u_ret/2.
The endpoint difference is

    v_k-v_0=-(2+sqrt(2))*e_ret-3u_ret/2.

Therefore the INITIAL-to-terminal charge is exactly

    (2+sqrt(2))^2+9/4
                  -(2+sqrt(2))*D_long+(3/4)*E_suffix. (35C)

By E_suffix<A_half, (25), 2+sqrt(2)>3 and 2+sqrt(2)<7/2,

    (35C)<15-(21/25)A_half+(3/4)A_half
          =15-(9/100)A_half<0,                      (36C)

because A_half>200.

Thus checking first, last, all intermediate adjacent pairs, AND the
original initial-terminal pair would still miss the next defect.

### 8.3 Reprove the newly decisive central-query-to-terminal cycle

The central QUERY is the original sample ell, immediately before its
actual central step B_mid, after the complete first H_(m-1).
Its gradient is v_ell=e_ret. No state is returned to a prior checkpoint.

From this central query to the terminal point, the FULL remaining
physical word is

    (B_mid,H_(m-1)),

in its original order with the same enclosing m.
Every late return coefficient in(2C) contributes to its displacement:

    D_k-D_ell=(B_mid-T_curve)*e_ret-E_suffix*u_ret,
    T_curve=sum_(r<ell)b_r*ell_curve(z_r)
                                   >=sqrt(2)*A_half. (37C)

The terminal difference from the central query gradient is

    v_k-v_ell=-(1+sqrt(2))*e_ret-u_ret.

Substitution into the ORIGINAL remote two-row identity(11) gives

    [Q_(ell,k)+Q_(k,ell)]/sigma^2
      =(1+sqrt(2))^2+1
                  +(1+sqrt(2))*(T_curve-B_mid)+E_suffix>0. (38C)

The strict sign follows from(23):

    T_curve>=sqrt(2)*A_half>B_mid,

and E_suffix>0. No terminal reset, expectation, Gram modification,
common-value choice, initial anchor or minimizer translation changes
this charge. The point displacement in(37C) contains every positive
suffix step.

The charge is quantitatively larger than A_half: since B_mid<A_half,
(1+sqrt(2))*(T_curve-B_mid)
 >(1+sqrt(2))*(sqrt(2)-1)*A_half=A_half.
This is a full-suffix payment failure, not a vanishing endpoint reset.

In particular the long curved return has paid its local slope changes
but RETURNED MORE longitudinal displacement than the central step
initially supplied. The gradient retained at the terminal has the
opposite longitudinal sign from the central query, and the resulting
remote pairing has the wrong sign for co-coercivity.

Law C is therefore impossible on EVERY stated H_m, despite all the
consecutive and initial-terminal two-row tests in8.1-8.2 passing.
This is a necessary full-row failure, not an upper bound on W(H_m).

## 9. Original benchmark and true-radius accounting

Every A,B,C attempt on(18) has a positive sum of two original rows.
Therefore at least one original row is positive, for EVERY sigma>0
and every choice of values. The static realization guard fails for
all three. The full all-word wrappers in4.3 select Huber BEFORE the run.

For these actual static wrapper functions, argmin={0}, x_0=1 and
the TRUE squared closest radius is1. Equations(16),(17) give

    V_wrapper(H_m)=1/(1+2S_m).                       (39)

Retain the ORIGINAL all-split benchmark:

    Omega(a,d)=(a+d+sqrt((a+d)^2+4ad))/2,
    G(a,d)=2a+d/2+sqrt(d^2+8ad)/2,
    C_0^bench=B_0^bench=1,
    C_n^bench=max_(0<=r<n)Omega(C_r^bench,C_(n-r-1)^bench),
    B_n^bench=max_(0<=r<n)G(C_r^bench,B_(n-r-1)^bench),
    U_n^ZJ=1/B_n^bench.                              (40)

Accepted5fd396eba75651e7, equation(77), applies to the SAME word(18)
at the SAME actual horizon n_m=2^m-1 and gives

    B_(n_m)^bench*V_wrapper(H_m)<237/250,
                                         m>=1536. (41)

No benchmark recurrence or split search was run. This is a fixed
relative deficit of these stated witness wrappers, not a deficit
of unrestricted W, and not a negative minimax certificate.

For the impossible memory attempts there is NO admissible function,
so there is no genuine minimizer set or true radius to normalize.
We do not divide their nominal terminal values by37 and call it a
witness. The impossibility itself is radius-independent by(11).
For any successful strict guarded member on another word, Section4.1
establishes its actual unique minimum and radius sqrt(37), without
assuming such a member exists or asserting a same-word improvement.

## 10. Failure synthesis and exact remaining interface

The three laws successively repair distinct original obligations:

A: final retention pays last and initial-terminal cycles, but
   variable early pair ratios increase the first gradient.
B: constant early coefficients pay first, and a word-dependent
   endpoint fit pays last, but every rebuilt negative suffix introduces
   earlier positive late cycles.
C: nonlinear longitudinal matching pays ALL those actual local returns
   and the initial-terminal cycle, but the FULL central-to-terminal
   displacement fails a different original remote cycle.

The shared obstacle is not an inability to choose a real Gram:
all gradients and points are explicit in a fixed real Euclidean plane.
It is simultaneous compatibility of longitudinal return payments over
all prefixes with the terminal jet and every remote directed row.
A payment paid on each adjacent step may spend more total displacement
than the central query can support.

These failures exclude only the displayed coefficient laws.
They do not exclude all nonclosing terminal memory, other signed
suffix laws, other curves, truly multi-mode geometries, or general
smooth-convex functions. No arbitrary-word optimality claim is made.

A next viable mechanism must specify the entire return schedule and
pay, at least, every first/last local cycle and each central-query
remote cycle with its FULL remaining displacement. It must also pay
all ORIGINAL directed/optimum rows and retain the true closest radius.
Keeping a terminal memory is not by itself sufficient.

The uniform original target still requires a static legal lower witness
for EVERY sufficiently large horizon and EVERY nonnegative word,
with payoff at least(1-o(1))*U_n^ZJ and a word-independent remainder.
Any universal comparison for a future family remains THEOREM-LEVEL.
A negative resolution still requires an all-function objective upper
certificate with fixed improvement on unbounded actual horizons.
This manuscript supplies neither. Original Goal ACTIVE/OPEN.

No formal submission, audit endorsement, publication, finalization,
revocation, shutdown, original-Goal completion or automatic renewal is
claimed or authorized by this one invocation.

## 11. Retrieval, authority, and resource record

## 12. Local glossary

h,n,S: full physical word, horizon and total mass.
k,i_r,b_r,j(i): positive-step count, original positive indices, actual
positive step values and count of preceding positive steps.
ell,r': early half-size and paired late query index.

with B_mid=0 when no middle step exists.

full weighted suffix return sum in Section2.

scale rule, arbitrary positive scale for screen scope, actual prefix
displacement and full sample vectors in Section4.

passing transcripts only.
Hub_delta,delta: separate canonical fallback function and its threshold.
lambda,u_m,beta_word,c_word,a_word,H_j,S_j,n_m: unchanged enclosing-m
tau1/4 word parameters, actual words, masses and physical horizons.
d_word,B_j: growth excess c_word-2 and the actual level-j step, distinct
from chronological b_r.
dL,dT: A's actual first gradient coefficient changes.
a_end: B's terminal difference factor q_end-3/2.
z,w,d,L_z,L_w,a: Section8.1 old/new scalar memories, increment,
