# Static noncommutative piecewise-quadratic fields: three exact failed screens

Project gd-exact-profile-20260926; worker max4; assigned analytical round66.
Status: one joint UNVERIFIED mathematical draft. No new fact_id, formal
submission, mathematical program, numerical evaluation, recurrence execution,
optimizer, installation, consult or subagent. This document closes only the
three specified screens. The original exact-profile minimax problem is OPEN.

## 1. Target, accepted interfaces, and the exact changed mechanism

The original target is to decide whether
lim_n W_n^*/U_n^ZJ=1, where W_n^*=inf_(h>=0) W_n(h) and
U_n^ZJ=1/B_n=1/(1+2S_bullet(n)), in the normalization
2(f(x_n)-min f)/(L*||x_0-x_star||^2). A matching affirmative proof requires
EVERY sufficiently large horizon and EVERY predetermined nonnegative word,
with a remainder independent of that word. None is supplied here.

Accepted dfde2fa326c908bf supplies full finite interpolation and the scoped
universal-lens exclusion. Accepted 1252b8823bb5a290 supplies maximal terminal
completion and the precise original directed residual. Neither fact says
that a freely prescribed switching Hessian sequence is realizable.
Accepted b98bb807d514e80d supplies the original benchmark definitions,
B_n<=1+4n^p with p=log_2(3), and the legal same-word Huber reference
H_base(h)=1/(1+2*sum h). Accepted 5fd396eba75651e7 supplies, on the current
enclosing-m word at tau=1/4 and every m>=1536,

    B_(2^m-1)*H_base(H_(m,1/4))<237/250.                 (1)

Only this scoped Huber comparison is used from the spiral fact. No primitive
history, negative minimax resolution, seed existence or discarded-prefix
claim is assumed.

The dispatch-specified shared drafts 687befe50820df21, 6e098f91c4a13444,
22347ffeb25f8830, d4ae45931cfc72a3, 97f52e264069f3ac,
8c09ef072ab9d6f9 and 5f198236d1ff200e were recalled from global memory.
They are UNVERIFIED awareness, not correctness premises. The first normal
and Jordan obstructions use c=13/5 and a=c^-17 or c^-18 with NO added1
in their bridge steps. The later near-unit Newton draft uses its own
tau=1 enclosing family. Those are not the current tau1/4 word.

The new controlling object is a SINGLE static convex C1 function with a
finite polyhedral partition. Its actual gradient changes according to
inequalities evaluated at the actual current point. Regional Hessians can
fail to commute, and the GD updates form an ordered operator word whose
letters are generated by these inequalities. No Hessian is assigned freely.
The constructions below all have explicit feedback or a reverse-side
reaction; none transports mass outward through disjoint tree generations.

For comparison, shared 89154918c70588a0/62e1d3a5b912ecbb studied a fixed
triangle-plane identity decomposition. Shared49c229ff9ff6eb2c/
0458dd4dceadebde studied outward64-ary Huber transport with pure-generation
dilution. Here there is no triangle incidence identity and no generation
splitting. The two oblique normals can return activity to an earlier side;
the third family uses coupled metric projection. Their failures are derived
again below, without using those unverified claims as lemmas.

## 2. Uniform notation, diagnostic word, and normalization

All spaces are finite-dimensional real Euclidean spaces. An operator norm
is the induced Euclidean norm. Positive part is z_+=max(z,0), and
negative part is z_-=max(-z,0). The superscript or subscript66 identifies
new local notation, distinct from the registered benchmark symbols.

Fix an integer m>=3 and 0<theta_66<=1/4. Put

    lambda=1+sqrt(2), beta_66=1-theta_66/m,
    c_66=lambda*(1+theta_66/m), a_66=beta_66/c_66,
    H^66_0=(), H^66_(j+1)=(H^66_j,b^66_j,H^66_j),
    b^66_j=1+a_66*c_66^j, n_m=2^m-1,
    S^66_m=sum H^66_m.

Every child uses this SAME enclosing (m,theta_66). Thus H^66_m is precisely
the current H_(m,tau) with tau=theta_66. No recursive child resets m.
The parameter theta_66 is unrelated to the project's Huber threshold tau.
Write A_66=b^66_0, B_66=b^66_1, C_66=b^66_2. The first four physical
steps of H^66_m are exactly (A_66,B_66,A_66,C_66).

The following rational bounds will be used, without numerical evaluation:

    4/3<A_66<sqrt(2)<17/12<3/2,
    23/12<=B_66<2, 3<C_66<4.                          (2)

Indeed beta_66>=11/12, c_66<=13lambda/12, and lambda<5/2 imply
a_66>=11/(13lambda)>22/65>1/3. Also
a_66<1/lambda=sqrt(2)-1. Further B_66=1+beta_66, and
C_66=1+lambda*(1-theta_66^2/m^2). Since lambda>12/5 and
theta_66^2/m^2<=1/144, its added term is at least143/60>2 and is
less than lambda<5/2. The square of17/12 exceeds2, giving its bound
on sqrt(2). This establishes (2).

For a function G with minimum0 and a specified initial point outside its
minimum set define

    E_66(G,h)=2G(x_n)/dist(x_0,argmin G)^2,
    x_(t+1)=x_t-h_t*grad G(x_t).                       (3)

The advertised smoothness is1. For every L>0 use f=L*G and the original
physical steps h_t/L. The iterates, true radius and normalized payoff are
unchanged. Every function below is static before this run; its dependence
on the known word is permitted by the original minimax quantifiers.

## 3. A complete scalar-to-field identity paying ALL directed rows

For 0<=eta<=1 define the single scalar C1 piecewise quadratic

    chi_eta(z)=(z_+^2+eta*z_-^2)/2,
    ell_eta(z)=chi_eta'(z)=z if z>=0, eta*z if z<=0.

These two definitions agree at0. The derivative is nondecreasing and
1-Lipschitz, so chi_eta is convex and C1. For any real s,t define

    J_eta(s,t)=chi_eta(s)-chi_eta(t)-ell_eta(t)*(s-t)
                         -(ell_eta(s)-ell_eta(t))^2/2.

Here is the exact exhaustive sign calculation:

    s,t>=0: J_eta=0;
    s,t<=0: J_eta=eta*(1-eta)*(s-t)^2/2;
    s>=0>=t: J_eta=eta*(1-eta)*t^2/2;
    s<=0<=t: J_eta=(1-eta)*(eta*s^2/2-s*t).             (4)

All four quantities are nonnegative. Endpoints satisfy adjacent formulas.
No scalar interpolation theorem is being imported.

Let T_66 be any real matrix with ||T_66||<=1, let d_66^aff be any fixed
offset vector, and define G(x)=sum_l chi_eta((T_66*x-d_66^aff)_l).
Put z_x=T_66*x-d_66^aff and v_x=(ell_eta((z_x)_l))_l. Then
grad G(x)=T_66^T*v_x, and

    ||grad G(x)-grad G(y)||<=||v_x-v_y||
                         <=||T_66*(x-y)||<=||x-y||.

This proves global1-smoothness, including all partition boundaries. On an
open sign cell the Hessian is T_66^T*D*T_66, where the diagonal entries
of D are1 or eta. Consequently0<=Hessian<=I. Convexity also follows
directly from convex chi_eta composed with affine forms.

For ANY x,y, in the original directed orientation of1252b8823bb5a290,

    Q^66_xy=G(x)-G(y)+<grad G(x),y-x>
                          +||grad G(x)-grad G(y)||^2/2
     =-sum_l J_eta((z_y)_l,(z_x)_l)
       -(||v_y-v_x||^2-||T_66^T*(v_y-v_x)||^2)/2<=0.   (5)

This identity pays every ordered pair on the SAME function. In particular
it pays pairs of remote queries, pairs in either orientation at a zero
step, every unqueried terminal pair, and every pair with ANY minimizer.
The Gram matrix of any finite actual sample collection is PSD because its
vectors are actual Euclidean vectors. There is no expected-only or
componentwise replacement for joint legality.

For the two fields below take, in R^2,

    u_66=(1,0), v_66=(-1/2,sqrt(3)/2), r_66=1/2,
    w_66=2/3=1/(1+r_66),
    p_66(x)=<u_66,x>, q_66(x)=<v_66,x>,
    U_66=u_66*u_66^T, V_66=v_66*v_66^T,
    T_66*x=sqrt(w_66)*(p_66(x),q_66(x)).

The Gram eigenvalues of the two unit normals are1-r_66 and1+r_66;
therefore ||T_66||^2=w_66*(1+r_66)=1. Set d_66^aff=0. The resulting
static field is

    F^66_eta(x)=w_66/2*[p_66(x)_+^2+q_66(x)_+^2
                         +eta*p_66(x)_-^2+eta*q_66(x)_-^2]. (6)

This is a finite sum of FOUR fixed hinge squares when eta>0 and of TWO
when eta=0. All normals, offsets0 and nonnegative weights are explicit.
Because chi_eta is homogeneous of degree2, (6) equals the G in (5).
The gradient is w_66[ell_eta(p_66)*u_66+ell_eta(q_66)*v_66].
Its open-cell Hessians are

    A^66_++=w_66*(U_66+V_66),
    A^66_+-=w_66*(U_66+eta*V_66),
    A^66_-+=w_66*(eta*U_66+V_66),
    A^66_--=eta*w_66*(U_66+V_66).                     (7)

The actual region is determined by the current signs of p_66,q_66.
All first two fields' updates are EXACTLY x_+=(I-h*A^66_sign(x))*x.
Since [U_66,V_66]=-r_66*(u_66*v_66^T-v_66*u_66^T)!=0,

    [A^66_+-,A^66_-+]=w_66^2*(1-eta^2)*[U_66,V_66]!=0

when eta<1. The two regions (++ and --) are nonempty; for (++), use
u_66+v_66. On this open region A^66_++ has eigenvalue1. Thus the
least global Lipschitz gradient constant of each (6) is exactly1,
not a loose trace normalization. For eta>0 the Hessians are also at
least (eta/3)*I, although this lower bound is not used for the payoff.

## 4. Screen1: obtuse two-hinge activation feedback, then exact absorption

Freeze eta=0 in (6) and start from x_0=u_66. The complete minimum set is

    K_66={x:p_66(x)<=0, q_66(x)<=0}, min F^66_0=0.     (8)

It contains0. For z in K_66, <u_66,z><=0, so
||u_66-z||^2=1+||z||^2-2<u_66,z>>=1. Equality holds only atz=0.
Thus the closest minimizer is0 and the true initial radius is exactly1.

Put d_66=1-2A_66/3, e_66=2B_66/3-1, t_66=d_66*e_66. By (2),

    d_66>1/18, e_66>=5/18, t_66>5/324.               (9)

The first four initialized updates, derived from their actual signs, are

    x_1=d_66*u_66,
    x_2=-t_66*u_66,
    x_3=-t_66*(u_66+(A_66/3)*v_66),
    x_4=-t_66*(u_66+((A_66+C_66*d_66)/3)*v_66).       (10)

Indeed at x_0,x_1 one has p_66>0,q_66<0, so the gradient is
(2/3)*p_66*u_66. At x_2, p_66=-t_66<0 and q_66=t_66/2>0,
so the gradient is (t_66/3)*v_66. At x_3,

    p_66=-t_66*(1-A_66/6)<0,
    q_66=t_66*d_66/2>0,

giving the fourth line of (10). At its end,

    p_66(x_4)=-t_66*[1-(A_66+C_66*d_66)/6]<0,
    q_66(x_4)=t_66*d_66*(1-2C_66/3)/2<0.            (11)

For the first sign, A_66<3/2, C_66<4 and0<d_66<1 give
A_66+C_66*d_66<11/2<6. For the second, C_66>3. This proves
the complete reached sequence of open regions

    (+,-), (+,-), (-,+), (-,+), (-,-).

The ordered product through x_4 is

    (I-C_66*w_66*V_66)*(I-A_66*w_66*V_66)
          *(I-B_66*w_66*U_66)*(I-A_66*w_66*U_66).    (12)

The U/V factors do not commute. These letters came from (10)--(11),
not from a postulated matrix word. This is real backward activity:
overshooting the u side activates the opposing oblique v side.
But x_4 is in the INTERIOR of the complete minimum cone. Every subsequent
gradient is exactly0, so EVERY remaining original step is executed at
the same point. The terminal objective is exactly0 for every m>=3.

The all-row identity (5), including (8)'s minimizers and terminalx_4,
is explicit. This is a legal, sharply1-smooth, actually noncommuting field,
but an exact failed screen: E_66(F^66_0,H^66_m)=0. This conclusion applies
to THIS initialization and THESE normals/weights/offsets, not to all
one-sided hinges or every initial point in this field.

## 5. Screen2: weak reverse-side curvature prevents zero payoff but confines it

Freeze, before the run, the explicit word-dependent number

    eta_66=1/[100000*(1+S^66_m)]                       (13)

in (6), and keep x_0=u_66. This permits the previously inactive side to
react, so it is a concrete change to the pure two-hinge law. Every term is
nonnegative, eta_66>0, and u_66,v_66 spanR^2. Thus the complete minimum
set is {0}, its attained value is0, and the true initial radius is1.
Equations (4)--(7) prove sharp global1-smoothness and ALL original rows.

The following initialization argument proves the switches rather than
assuming them persist under a perturbation. For either field (6), the
piecewise-affine continuous gradient along a line segment is the integral
of symmetric Hessians between0 andI. Segments lying in a boundary are
handled by splitting the finitely many affine scalar forms, whose slope
can be chosen on the agreeing boundary; the gradient remains continuous.
Consequently the GD map has global Lipschitz constant at most

    k_h=max(1,|1-h|) for h>=0,                         (14)

because its difference at two points is (I-h*Abar)*(x-y), where
0<=Abar<=I. Both maps fix0. Further

    ||grad F^66_eta(x)-grad F^66_0(x)||<=eta*||x||.     (15)

To see (15), write the difference as eta*T_66^T times the negative
coordinates of T_66*x, retaining their signed values, and use ||T_66||=1.

Let x_t^eta be the actual trajectory for (13) and x_t^0 the trajectory
(10). These are two fixed functions, not a time-varying blend. Since
A_66,B_66,A_66<2, (14) gives ||x_t^eta||<=1 for0<=t<=3.
Comparison using the F_0 map and (15) yields, successively,

    ||x_1^eta-x_1^0||<(3/2)*eta_66,
    ||x_2^eta-x_2^0||<(7/2)*eta_66,
    ||x_3^eta-x_3^0||<5*eta_66,
    ||x_4^eta-x_4^0||<19*eta_66, ||x_4^eta||<3.       (16)

The fourth estimate uses k_(C_66)<3, C_66<4 and
||x_3^eta||<=1. No recurrence or trajectory was executed by a program.

Every absolute residual p_66 or q_66 at x_t^0,1<=t<=4, is greater
than1/2400. For t=1,2 this follows from (9) and residuals
(d_66,-d_66/2), (-t_66,t_66/2). For t=3 the smaller relevant residual
is q_66=t_66*d_66/2>5/11664>1/2400, and
|p_66|>3t_66/4. For t=4, (11) gives
|p_66|>t_66/12>5/3888 and
|q_66|>t_66*d_66/2>5/11664. These are exact rational comparisons.
Both normals have norm1; (13),(16) and19/100000<1/2400 prove that
the actual perturbed signs at every one of these points agree with those
in Screen1. In particular x_4^eta is in the interior of K_66.

Thus the ACTUAL first ordered product is, with the matrices from (7)
evaluated at eta_66,

    x_4^eta=(I-C_66*A^66_-+)*(I-A_66*A^66_-+)
                 *(I-B_66*A^66_+-)*(I-A_66*A^66_+-)*u_66. (17)

It is genuinely noncommutative. The field has positive curvature and
gradient in the (--)-region, so that region is no longer a minimum cone.
Its continued chronology must still be proved. Inside K_66 the residual
update under any remaining original step h is

    (p_66(x_+),q_66(x_+))^T
      =[[1-eta_66*w_66*h, eta_66*w_66*r_66*h],
        [eta_66*w_66*r_66*h, 1-eta_66*w_66*h]]
                                  *(p_66(x),q_66(x))^T. (18)

Each h<=S^66_m and (13) gives eta_66*w_66*h<1. Every matrix entry
in (18) is nonnegative, and its diagonal entries are strictly positive.
Hence BOTH residuals remain strictly negative forever. No positive-side
hinge reactivates. Every remaining operator is exactly
I-h*eta_66*A^66_++, and

    x_(n_m)^eta=product_(t=4)^(n_m-1)(I-h_t*eta_66*A^66_++)*x_4^eta. (19)

This tail product commutes because its confinement was proved. Its
eigenvalues are1-h*eta_66 and1-h*eta_66/3, both in(0,1]; thus
||x_(n_m)^eta||<3. The EXACT terminal value in this reached region is

    2F^66_eta66(x_(n_m)^eta)
          =eta_66*<x_(n_m)^eta,A^66_++*x_(n_m)^eta>.

It is strictly positive and at most9*eta_66, so

    E_66(F^66_eta66,H^66_m)<9/[100000*(1+S^66_m)],
    E_66/H_base(H^66_m)<18/100000=9/50000.             (20)

For theta_66=1/4 and EVERY m>=1536, (1) gives the original benchmark
comparison E_66/U_(n_m)^ZJ<(9/50000)*(237/250)<1/5000.
There is no omitted true-radius factor. This rejects the specific weak
reverse-curvature law early and uniformly. It DOES NOT exclude larger
reverse weights, other initializations, affine offsets, different normal
angles or fields that reactivate their strong-curvature side.

## 6. Screen3: a polyhedral squared-distance field with actual repeated feedback

Here use new normals with a sharper metric coupling:

    r_66^D=sqrt(15)/4, delta_66^D=1-(r_66^D)^2=1/16,
    u_66^D=(1,0), v_66^D=(-r_66^D,1/4),
    U_66^D=u_66^D*(u_66^D)^T, V_66^D=v_66^D*(v_66^D)^T,
    p_66^D(x)=<u_66^D,x>, q_66^D(x)=<v_66^D,x>,
    K_66^D={x:p_66^D(x)<=0,q_66^D(x)<=0},
    D_66(x)=dist(x,K_66^D)^2/2.                       (21)

This is ONE static globally C1 convex piecewise-quadratic function, not
a sum of independently chosen face energies. To remove boundary ambiguity
in the initialized Hessians, freeze epsilon_66^D=1/100, put

    k_66^D=-epsilon_66^D*(v_66^D+r_66^D*u_66^D),
    x_0^D=u_66^D+k_66^D.                              (22)

Then k_66^D is in K_66^D, p_66^D(k_66^D)=0 and
q_66^D(k_66^D)=-epsilon_66^D*delta_66^D<0. For z in K_66^D,
<u_66^D,z-k_66^D><=0, giving
||x_0^D-z||^2>=||u_66^D||^2=1, with equality atz=k_66^D.
The actual closest initial radius is therefore1, although ||x_0^D||>1.
The complete minimum set is K_66^D and its attained value is0.

### 6.1 The complete static regions and update guards

For P_D=p_66^D(x), Q_D=q_66^D(x), r_D=r_66^D, the exhaustive region law is

    K: P_D<=0,Q_D<=0                     => grad D_66=0, D_66=0;
    U: P_D>=0,Q_D+r_D*P_D<=0             => grad D_66=P_D*u_66^D, D_66=P_D^2/2;
    V: Q_D>=0,P_D+r_D*Q_D<=0             => grad D_66=Q_D*v_66^D, D_66=Q_D^2/2;
    O: P_D+r_D*Q_D>=0,Q_D+r_D*P_D>=0     => grad D_66=x, D_66=||x||^2/2. (23)

On overlaps the value and gradient formulas agree. In regionU,
x-P_D*u_66^D lies in K_66^D and P_D*u_66^D is an outward normal there;
similarly for V. In regionO,

    x=[(P_D+r_D*Q_D)/(1-r_D^2)]*u_66^D
                     +[(Q_D+r_D*P_D)/(1-r_D^2)]*v_66^D

is in the nonnegative normal cone at0, so its closest point is0.
These conditions partition the plane: if neither K nor a feasible
one-wall projection applies, both displayed coefficients are nonnegative.
Thus (23) is an exact projection law. The open-cell Hessians are respectively
0,U_66^D,V_66^D,I. These have norm at most1, and the nonempty open O
region makes the least Lipschitz gradient constant exactly1.
The actual next iterate is (I-h*A^D_66(x))*x, with A chosen by (23).
This guard, evaluated at every actual state, specifies the entire later
chronology; no later face sequence is being prescribed independently.

### 6.2 Initialized U-to-V-to-U-to-V feedback is actually reached

In this paragraph use r_D=r_66^D, delta_D=delta_66^D,
epsilon_D=epsilon_66^D, P_(D,t)=p_66^D(x_t^D), Q_(D,t)=q_66^D(x_t^D).
Put alpha=A_66-1>1/3 and F_66^D=B_66*r_D^2-1>=51/64.
At x_0^D, P_(D,0)=1 and Q_(D,0)+r_D*P_(D,0)=-epsilon_D*delta_D<0,
so it is in OPEN U.
At x_1^D=(I-A_66*U_66^D)*x_0^D,

    P_(D,1)=-alpha, Q_(D,1)=r_D*alpha-epsilon_D*delta_D>0,
    P_(D,1)+r_D*Q_(D,1)=-delta_D*(alpha+r_D*epsilon_D)<0.

The next query is therefore in OPEN V. The second update gives

    P_(D,2)=alpha*F_66^D-B_66*r_D*epsilon_D*delta_D>0,
    Q_(D,2)=(1-B_66)*Q_(D,1)<0,
    Q_(D,2)+r_D*P_(D,2)
       =-delta_D*[B_66*r_D*alpha+epsilon_D*(1-B_66*delta_D)]<0.

For its strict positive residual, P_(D,2)>17/64-2/1600>0. It is in OPEN U.
The third update gives

    P_(D,3)=(1-A_66)*P_(D,2)<0,
    Q_(D,3)=r_D*alpha*[A_66*F_66^D-(B_66-1)]
          -epsilon_D*delta_D*[A_66*B_66*r_D^2-(B_66-1)]>0,
    P_(D,3)+r_D*Q_(D,3)
       =-delta_D*[alpha*(1+A_66*F_66^D)
                      +r_D*epsilon_D*(1-A_66*B_66*delta_D)]<0. (24)

To check Q_(D,3)>0 completely, the expression
A*(15B/16-1)-(B-1) increases in both A and B on (2)'s rectangle.
At A=4/3,B=23/12 its value is7/48. Since r_D>1/2 and alpha>1/3,
the first term in Q_(D,3) is greater than7/288. The subtracted term is
less than3/1600, which is smaller than7/288. Also
A_66*B_66*delta_D<3/16<1, proving the last sign in (24).
The query x_3^D is in OPEN V. Thus the actually reached first three
update factors are

    x_3^D=(I-A_66*U_66^D)*(I-B_66*V_66^D)
                         *(I-A_66*U_66^D)*x_0^D.     (25)

Here [U_66^D,V_66^D]=-r_D*(u_66^D*(v_66^D)^T-v_66^D*(u_66^D)^T)!=0.
This family really returns to an earlier active wall, instead of being
a relabeled outward graph. Later queries use all four guards (23).
The following bound applies to every possible guard-generated later
history, so a complete list of its later faces is not needed to reject it.

### 6.3 Global1-smoothness and exact ALL-directed legality, without an external premise

The argument works for ANY nonempty closed convex set K in any finite
Euclidean dimension, with D(x)=dist(x,K)^2/2. Write P_x for its metric
projection and R_x=x-P_x. Existence follows by minimizing the continuous
coercive squared distance on the closed set; strict convexity along the
segment between two distinct candidates gives uniqueness. Differentiating
the squared distance along P_x+t*(z-P_x) at t=0 gives

    <R_x,z-P_x><=0 for EVERY z in K.                  (26)

The two projection inequalities at x,y give
<R_x-R_y,P_x-P_y>>=0. Since x-y=(P_x-P_y)+(R_x-R_y),

    <R_x-R_y,x-y>>=||R_x-R_y||^2,
    ||R_x-R_y||<=||x-y||.                             (27)

Convex combinations of P_x,P_y are in K, and convexity of squared norm
shows D is convex. Evaluating the squared distance to P_x at x+d gives
D(x+d)<=D(x)+<R_x,d>+||d||^2/2. Direct expansion gives

    D(y)-D(x)-<R_x,y-x>
           =||R_y-R_x||^2/2-<R_x,P_y-P_x>>=0.        (28)

These lower and upper remainder bounds prove differentiability with
grad D(x)=R_x; (27) proves global1-Lipschitz gradient. The entire original
ordered interpolation residual is the EXACT expression

    D(x)-D(y)+<R_x,y-x>+||R_x-R_y||^2/2
                             =<R_x,P_y-P_x><=0.      (29)

Equation (26) proves its sign for all x,y, including every remote query,
the actual terminal and any minimizer. The complete Gram is again actual
Euclidean data. If y is any minimizer, P_y=y and R_y=0; the opposite
minimizer-to-query residual is exactly0. Nothing is paid only in expectation.

### 6.4 A dimension-free exact product bound rejects this WHOLE unscaled family

For any nonnegative step h and current pointx,

    x_+=x-h*R_x=P_x+(1-h)*R_x,
    dist(x_+,K)<=|1-h|*dist(x,K).                    (30)

The comparison point P_x is in K. This proves (30) even when the update
crosses arbitrarily many faces, and even for h>2 or h=0. Induction along
the ORIGINAL full physical word gives

    E_66(D,H^66_m)
       =dist(x_(n_m),K)^2/dist(x_0,K)^2
       <=product_(t<n_m)|1-h_t|^2
       =beta_66^(2n_m)*c_66^(-2m).                  (31)

For the last equality, levelj has multiplicity2^(m-j-1), the total
count is n_m, and the sum of j times these multiplicities is n_m-m.
Both sums follow inductively from the actual recursive word. Since
|1-b_j^66|=a_66*c_66^j, their product is
a_66^n_m*c_66^(n_m-m)=beta_66^n_m*c_66^-m. These are symbolic
identities; the word and product were not executed by a program.

Accepted b98bb807d514e80d gives B_n<=1+4n^p, p=log_2(3).
Since n_m<2^m and0<beta_66<1, (31) gives uniformly

    B_(n_m)*E_66(D,H^66_m)
       <=(1+4*3^m)*c_66^(-2m)
       <=5*(3/lambda^2)^m ->0,                      (32)

because lambda^2=3+2sqrt(2)>3. This bound is uniform in the dimension,
the nonempty closed convex set, its offsets and facial angles, the initial
point outside it, and0<theta_66<=1/4. In particular it covers every
unscaled polyhedral squared-distance C1 field of the assigned type.
Increasing the number of faces, using overlapping real incidence geometry,
or increasing actual Hessian noncommutation cannot defeat (30).

Even the full SAME-word Huber reference dominates asymptotically. The exact
sum identity is

    S^66_m=n_m+a_66*(c_66^m-2^m)/(c_66-2)<2*c_66^m,

because c_66>lambda>2 and
a_66/(c_66-2)<(1/lambda)/(lambda-2)=1. Thus
E_66/H_base<5*beta_66^(2n_m)*c_66^-m<=5*c_66^-m->0.

The exclusion is for D=dist^2/2 at advertised smoothness1. A horizon-tuned
smaller scalar multiple, a Moreau envelope of a non-indicator function,
general sums of unequal-distance penalties or affine hinge feedback are
outside (30)'s hypotheses. There is NO upper bound on unrestrictedW_n.

## 7. What the three screens decide, and the next theorem-level interface

| Static field and start | Actual changing curvature | Full minimum set / true radius | Paid terminal comparison | Exact scope of failure |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| F^66_0 from u_66 | U then V; noncommuting (12) | K_66; nearest0; radius1 | Exactly0 after fourth physical step | The two specified normals, weights, zero offsets and start |
| F^66_eta66 with frozen (13), from u_66 | Two different reverse-side Hessians in (17), then proven weak tail | {0}; radius1 | E/H_base<9/50000; at tau1/4,m>=1536, E/U<1/5000 | This weak reverse-curvature law and start |
| D_66 from (22), and whole unscaled squared-distance class | Reached U,V,U,V; later all static projection guards | Explicit K_66^D; closest k_66^D; radius1 | Whole-class E/U<=5*(3/lambda^2)^m->0 | Unscaled dist(x,K)^2/2 at advertised smoothness1 |

All three are actual STATIC, convex, globally C1, sharply1-smooth
piecewise-quadratic fields in their concrete planar realizations. Every
gradient, terminal value, minimum set, closest radius, chronological
switch and directed interpolation row has been paid. These finite exact
realizations are all quantitative failures as lower witnesses on the
current inherited-m family; there is no viable benchmark-scale candidate
among these THREE screened laws and no claimed all-word bridge.

The remaining interface for this program is quantitative re-entry into
strong curvature while retaining terminal objective value on growing
depths. In (18) the negative cone is forward invariant; in (30) the true
distance residual always contracts by |1-h|. A new field must explicitly
break the relevant invariant or leave the squared-distance structure.
Changing only the number of faces in Screen3 cannot help. Changing only
the positive field normalization while keeping exactly (13) cannot help.
Larger reverse-side weights, carefully paid affine offsets, other normal
couplings and non-indicator envelope pieces remain outside the failures.

A subsequent concrete proposal would need actual region inequalities for
each return, its full static norm bound, the exact retained terminal value
and true radius, and a quantitative all-depth estimate. It would then still
need a constant-preserving mechanism for arbitrary nonnegative words and
every large horizon; the H_m family is only a diagnostic. That last uniform
interface is a theorem-strength OPEN requirement, not an established
reformulation, a technical lemma or a minimax disproof. No fourth law is
screened or proposed as a solved candidate in this allocated round.

## 8. Primary-source retrieval and applicability audit

The active program was a mutation from fixed polynomial generators to
static sign-dependent fields; the missing mechanism was legal initialized
feedback with no radial/mass loss. Matlas was queried first for hinge-square
convexity and for squared-distance smoothness. Built-in web retrieval read
the exact papers and the relevant proof passages. The complete downloaded
HTML, PDF and administratively extracted text of each are in the public
source_documents/ subdirectory of this artifact directory.

1. Mert Gürbüzbalaban, Yuanhan Hu and Lingjiong Zhu, *Penalized Overdamped
   and Underdamped Langevin Monte Carlo Algorithms for Constrained Sampling*,
   arXiv:2212.00570v2 (2024). Read Section2.4, Lemma2.25 and its full
   appendix proof, and Lemma2.6 and its proof. Matlas returned the affine
   penalty result as Lemma2.23; the exact downloadedv2 numbers it2.25.
   Its penalty convention has no factor1/2. For affine forms its Hessian
   analysis explains the static hinge partition; its trace smoothness bound
   does not establish the sharp constant1 here. Equations (4)--(7) supply
   that constant and all boundary/directed-row checks directly.
   [Exact source](https://arxiv.org/html/2212.00570v2).

2. Michael I. Jordan, Tianyi Lin and Manolis Zampetakis, *First-Order
   Algorithms for Nonlinear Generalized Nash Equilibrium Problems*,
   arXiv:2204.03132v3 (2023), PropositionD.1 and LemmaD.2, including
   the latter proof. The former states the standard factor2 gradient and
   Lipschitz bound for squared distance, referring to another source for
   its proof. Here the factor1/2 is explicit, the set is nonempty closed
   convex, and (26)--(29) give a COMPLETE direct proof. No equilibrium,
   compactness, game-theoretic or penalty-algorithm convergence theorem is
   used. [Exact source](https://arxiv.org/html/2204.03132v3).

These references ground construction vocabulary; no external theorem is a
black-box premise of a new result. The sharper noncommutative feedback,
confinement, radius and product estimates are entirely displayed in this
joint draft. No external arithmetic or source's numerical results were used.

# A genuinely non-core binary staircase on the arithmetic-rank body

Project: gd-exact-profile-20260926. Worker: max. Allocated round: 64.
Originating guidance: 465affb3ea6cfb83. Frozen plan: b4edb4877ec644b4.

**Status: COMPLETE JOINT UNVERIFIED mathematical draft. No fact_id.**
The assignment forbids a formal gate. All new geometry, trajectory,
inequalities and limits below belong to this one joint proof attempt.
None is being represented as an accepted predecessor. The original
all-word, all-horizon exact minimax Goal remains OPEN.

The rank body is unchanged:

    F_d(A)=|A|(d-|A|)/d,
    K_d={g in R^d: sum_i g_i=0,
                  sum_(i in A) g_i<=F_d(A) for every A subset[d]}.

We freeze one initial profile in dimension d=2^m. Its actual trajectory
never enters K_d, including at the first update and at the terminal point.
It has m-1 distinct ordered-block transitions, and its gradients span an
m-dimensional space. Each transition is proved at its original physical
bridge. The terminal value nevertheless loses to the same-word scalar
Huber witness by a nonvanishing true-radius factor.

The exact limit of its normalized payoff V_(m,tau) is

    lim_(m->infinity) B_(2^m-1) V_(m,tau)
      = Z_1 exp(-tau) / [2(1+eta)],
    eta = 1/[2(4lambda^2-1)],  lambda=1+sqrt(2).       (A)

Convergence is uniform for tau in [1/5,1/4]. In that whole interval the
limit is strictly below 2976/2989<249/250, and eventually the actual
finite payoff satisfies B_(2^m-1)V_(m,tau)<249/250 uniformly in tau.
At tau=1/4 the limit is below 47616/50225<19/20, and the finite payoff
is eventually below 19/20. These are upper bounds on this constructed
family's payoff, not on the unrestricted worst-case functional W_n.
